
==================== FILE: ./scraper/booking_parser.py ====================

"""Dynamic Booking.com parser adhering to Zero-Update architecture and defensive parsing."""

from datetime import datetime
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import parse_qsl, urlencode, urlparse, urlunparse

from playwright.sync_api import Locator, Page

from config.settings import get_settings
from core.logger import logger
from core.models import RoomOffer, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob

SCREENSHOTS_DIR = get_settings().logs_dir / "screenshots"


def clean_price_text(raw_text: str, price_regex: Optional[str] = None) -> Optional[float]:
    """Extract and normalize a numeric price value from raw scraped text."""
    if not raw_text or not isinstance(raw_text, str):
        return None

    cleaned = raw_text.replace("\xa0", " ").replace("\u202f", " ").strip()

    if price_regex:
        match = re.search(price_regex, cleaned)
        if match:
            cleaned = match.group(1) if match.groups() else match.group(0)

    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^\d.,]", "", cleaned).strip()
    if not cleaned:
        return None

    try:
        if "," in cleaned and "." in cleaned:
            if cleaned.rfind(",") > cleaned.rfind("."):
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(".", "").replace(",", ".")
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", "")
        elif "," in cleaned:
            parts = cleaned.split(",")
            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[1]) in (1, 2):
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", ".")
            else:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(",", "")
        elif "." in cleaned:
            parts = cleaned.split(".")
            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[1]) in (1, 2):
                pass
            elif len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[1]) == 3:
                cleaned = cleaned.replace(".", "")

        return round(float(cleaned), 2)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
        logger.debug(f"Failed to parse cleaned price '{cleaned}': {exc}")
        return None


class BookingParser:
    """Dynamic extractor for Booking.com using remote rules and defensive parsing."""

    def __init__(self, screenshots_dir: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
        self.screenshots_dir = Path(screenshots_dir or SCREENSHOTS_DIR)
        self.screenshots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def build_booking_url(self, base_url: str, job: ScrapingJob) -> str:
        """Append check-in, check-out, and guest count parameters to Booking.com URL."""
        if not base_url:
            return ""
        parsed = urlparse(base_url)
        params = dict(parse_qsl(parsed.query))
        params["checkin"] = job.check_in.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        params["checkout"] = job.check_out.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        params["group_adults"] = str(job.adults)
        params["group_children"] = str(job.children)
        params["no_rooms"] = "1"
        new_query = urlencode(params)
        return urlunparse(parsed._replace(query=new_query))

    def build_catalog_booking_url(self, base_url: str) -> str:
        """Return base Booking.com URL stripped of date and guest parameters for catalog sync."""
        if not base_url:
            return ""
        parsed = urlparse(base_url)
        excluded = {
            "checkin",
            "checkout",
            "group_adults",
            "group_children",
            "no_rooms",
            "age",
        }
        filtered_params = [
            (k, v) for k, v in parse_qsl(parsed.query) if k.lower() not in excluded
        ]
        new_query = urlencode(filtered_params)
        return urlunparse(parsed._replace(query=new_query))

    def _save_diagnostic_screenshot(self, page: Page, hotel_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        """Safely capture a screenshot for debugging unidentified layouts."""
        try:
            safe_slug = re.sub(r"[^\w]+", "_", hotel_name.strip().lower()).strip("_")
            timestamp = datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            filename = f"booking_{safe_slug}_{timestamp}.png"
            target_path = self.screenshots_dir / filename
            page.screenshot(path=str(target_path), full_page=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved diagnostic screenshot to: {target_path}")
            return target_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(f"Could not save diagnostic screenshot for {hotel_name}: {exc}")
            return None

    def _dismiss_cookie_banners(self, page: Page, rules: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Dynamically dismiss cookie consent modals based on remote rules."""
        consent_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
            "cookie_consent_selectors",
            [
                "#onetrust-accept-btn-handler",
                "button#onetrust-accept-btn-handler",
                "button[id*='cookie'][id*='accept']",
                "button:has-text('Prijať všetky')",
                "button:has-text('Súhlasím')",
                "button:has-text('Accept all')",
            ],
        )
        for selector in consent_selectors:
            try:
                btn = page.locator(selector).first
                if btn.is_visible(timeout=1500):
                    btn.click(timeout=1500)
                    logger.debug(f"Dismissed cookie consent via selector: {selector}")
                    page.wait_for_timeout(500)
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue

    def _extract_occupancy(
        self,
        row: Locator,
        occupancy_selectors: List[str],
        icon_selectors: List[str],
    ) -> Optional[int]:
        """Extract number of guests (occupancy) from a room table row."""
        for occ_sel in occupancy_selectors:
            try:
                occ_cell = row.locator(occ_sel).first
                if occ_cell.count() == 0:
                    continue

                # 1. Kontrola počtu ikon osôb
                for icon_sel in icon_selectors:
                    icon_locator = occ_cell.locator(icon_sel)
                    icon_count = icon_locator.count()
                    if icon_count > 0:
                        return icon_count

                # 2. Kontrola aria-label alebo title atribútov
                for attr_name in ("aria-label", "title", "data-title"):
                    attr_val = occ_cell.get_attribute(attr_name)
                    if attr_val:
                        m = re.search(r"(\d+)", attr_val)
                        if m and int(m.group(1)) > 0:
                            return int(m.group(1))

                # 3. Kontrola textového obsahu bunky
                text_val = occ_cell.text_content()
                if text_val:
                    m = re.search(r"(\d+)", text_val)
                    if m and int(m.group(1)) > 0:
                        return int(m.group(1))

            except Exception:
                continue

        return None

    def _extract_bed_info(self, row: Locator, bed_selectors: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
        """Extract and join unique bed configuration texts from a room row."""
        for b_sel in bed_selectors:
            try:
                loc = row.locator(b_sel)
                count = loc.count()
                if count == 0:
                    continue
                parts: List[str] = []
                seen = set()
                for i in range(count):
                    raw_text = loc.nth(i).text_content()
                    if raw_text:
                        cleaned = " ".join(raw_text.split())
                        if cleaned and cleaned.lower() not in seen:
                            seen.add(cleaned.lower())
                            parts.append(cleaned)
                if parts:
                    return " + ".join(parts)
            except Exception:
                continue
        return None

    def _extract_bed_info_from_name_element(
        self,
        name_loc: Locator,
        bed_selectors: List[str],
        fallback_row: Optional[Locator] = None,
    ) -> Optional[str]:
        """Extract bed info from the nearest enclosing row/container of a room name element."""
        ancestor_xpaths = [
            "xpath=ancestor::tr[1]",
            "xpath=ancestor::div[.//span[contains(@class,'ddaf087582')] or .//*[@data-testid='bed-info']][1]",
            "xpath=ancestor::div[.//*[contains(@class,'ddaf087582')] or .//*[@data-testid='bed-info'] or .//*[contains(@class,'rt-bed-types')]][1]",
            "xpath=ancestor::*[self::tr or @data-testid='property-card' or contains(@class,'room-row')][1]",
        ]
        for anc_xpath in ancestor_xpaths:
            try:
                anc_loc = name_loc.locator(anc_xpath)
                if anc_loc.count() > 0:
                    bed_info = self._extract_bed_info(anc_loc, bed_selectors)
                    if not bed_info:
                        bed_info = self._extract_bed_info(anc_loc.first, bed_selectors)
                    if bed_info:
                        return bed_info
            except Exception:
                continue

        if fallback_row is not None:
            return self._extract_bed_info(fallback_row, bed_selectors)
        return None

    def parse_room_catalog(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
    ) -> List[RoomOffer]:
        """Parse undated Booking.com page to extract all available room types and bed info."""
        try:
            rules = rules or {}
            try:
                page.wait_for_load_state("domcontentloaded", timeout=10000)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug(f"domcontentloaded timeout for catalog {hotel_name}: {exc}")

            self._dismiss_cookie_banners(page, rules)

            default_room_name_selectors = [
                ".b08850ce41",
                ".hprt-roomtype-icon-link",
                ".hprt-roomtype-link",
                "[data-testid='room-name']",
                ".room_link span",
            ]
            raw_room_name_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "room_name_selectors", default_room_name_selectors
            )
            room_name_selectors = list(
                dict.fromkeys([*raw_room_name_selectors, *default_room_name_selectors])
            )

            default_bed_selectors = [
                ".ddaf087582",
                "[data-testid='bed-info']",
                ".rt-bed-types",
            ]
            raw_bed_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "bed_selectors", default_bed_selectors
            )
            bed_selectors = list(
                dict.fromkeys([*raw_bed_selectors, *default_bed_selectors])
            )

            wait_candidates = ", ".join(
                dict.fromkeys(
                    [
                        "table.hprt-table",
                        "[data-testid='property-section--rooms-table']",
                        *room_name_selectors,
                    ]
                )
            )
            try:
                page.wait_for_selector(wait_candidates, timeout=8000)
            except Exception:
                page.wait_for_timeout(1000)

            offers: List[RoomOffer] = []
            seen_pairs = set()

            rows = page.locator("tr, [data-testid='property-card'], li")
            row_count = rows.count()
            for idx in range(row_count):
                try:
                    row = rows.nth(idx)
                    room_name: Optional[str] = None
                    for n_sel in room_name_selectors:
                        try:
                            name_loc = row.locator(n_sel).first
                            if name_loc.count() > 0:
                                val = name_loc.text_content()
                                if val and val.strip():
                                    room_name = " ".join(val.split())
                                    break
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                    if not room_name:
                        continue

                    bed_info = self._extract_bed_info(row, bed_selectors)
                    if not bed_info:
                        continue

                    dedup_key = (room_name.lower(), bed_info.lower())
                    if dedup_key in seen_pairs:
                        continue
                    seen_pairs.add(dedup_key)

                    offers.append(
                        RoomOffer(
                            room_name=room_name,
                            price=0.0,
                            bed_info=bed_info,
                        )
                    )
                except Exception as row_exc:
                    logger.debug(
                        f"Chyba pri spracovaní katalógového riadku {idx} pre {hotel_name}: {row_exc}"
                    )
                    continue

            logger.info(
                f"Katalóg Booking pre '{hotel_name}': nájdených {len(offers)} unikátnych izieb."
            )
            return offers
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"Chyba pri parsovaní Booking katalógu pre {hotel_name}: {exc}")
            return []

    def parse_hotel_page(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
        requested_guests: int = 2,
    ) -> ScrapedResult:
        """Parse hotel details extracting ALL room offers matching requested occupancy."""
        try:
            # 0. Čakanie na DOM
            try:
                page.wait_for_load_state("domcontentloaded", timeout=10000)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug(f"domcontentloaded timeout for {hotel_name}: {exc}")

            # 1. Odkliknutie cookies
            self._dismiss_cookie_banners(page, rules)

            # 2. Čakanie na tabuľku alebo banner vypredania
            try:
                page.wait_for_selector(
                    "table.hprt-table, [data-testid='property-section--rooms-table'], .fe_banner__message, [data-testid='sold-out-property-banner']",
                    timeout=12000,
                )
            except Exception:
                page.wait_for_timeout(1500)

            # 3. Kontrola stavu vypredania
            sold_out_selectors: List[str] = rules.get("sold_out_selectors", [])
            for selector in sold_out_selectors:
                try:
                    locator = page.locator(selector)
                    count = locator.count()
                    if count > 0 and any(locator.nth(i).is_visible() for i in range(count)):
                        logger.info(f"Hotel {hotel_name} je na Bookingu vypredaný.")
                        return ScrapedResult(
                            hotel_name=hotel_name,
                            is_available=False,
                            booking_price=None,
                            offers=[],
                            error_message=None,
                        )
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Error checking sold_out_selector '{selector}': {exc}")

            # 4. Načítanie remote selektorov
            room_row_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "room_row_selectors",
                [
                    "table.hprt-table tbody tr",
                    "[data-testid='property-section--rooms-table'] tbody tr",
                    "tr[data-block-id]",
                ],
            )
            occupancy_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "occupancy_selectors",
                ["td.hprt-table-cell-occupancy", ".hprt-occupancy-occupancy-info", ".c-occupancy"],
            )
            occupancy_icon_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "occupancy_icon_selectors",
                [".bicon-occupancy", "i.bicon-occupancy", "span.bicon-occupancy", "svg"],
            )
            room_name_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "room_name_selectors",
                [
                    ".hprt-roomtype-icon-link",
                    ".hprt-roomtype-link",
                    "[data-testid='room-name']",
                    ".room_link span",
                    ".b08850ce41",
                ],
            )
            bed_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "bed_selectors",
                [".ddaf087582", "[data-testid='bed-info']", ".rt-bed-types"],
            )
            price_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "price_selectors",
                [
                    ".bui-price-display__value",
                    "[data-testid='price-and-discounted-final-price']",
                    ".prco-valign-middle-helper",
                    ".hprt-price-price",
                ],
            )
            meal_plan_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "meal_plan_selectors",
                [".hprt-condition-detail", ".hprt-table-cell-conditions", ".bui-list__item"],
            )
            cancellation_selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
                "cancellation_selectors",
                [".hprt-cancellation-policy", ".hprt-table-cell-conditions"],
            )
            price_regex: Optional[str] = rules.get("price_regex")

            matched_offers: List[RoomOffer] = []
            rows_detected_on_page = False

            # 5. Vyhľadanie riadkov cez fallback selektory
            for row_selector in room_row_selectors:
                try:
                    rows = page.locator(row_selector)
                    row_count = rows.count()
                    if row_count == 0:
                        continue

                    rows_detected_on_page = True
                    logger.debug(f"Pre {hotel_name} nájdených {row_count} riadkov cez selector '{row_selector}'")
                    current_room_name = "Štandardná izba"
                    last_known_occupancy = requested_guests
                    last_known_bed_info: Optional[str] = None

                    for idx in range(row_count):
                        try:
                            row = rows.nth(idx)

                            # A) Detekcia zmeny názvu izby (nový blok izby)
                            for n_sel in room_name_selectors:
                                name_loc = row.locator(n_sel).first
                                if name_loc.count() > 0:
                                    val = name_loc.text_content()
                                    if val and val.strip():
                                        current_room_name = val.strip()
                                        last_known_occupancy = requested_guests
                                        last_known_bed_info = None
                                        break

                            # A2) Extrakcia postelí s dedením pre cenové sub-riadky
                            extracted_beds = self._extract_bed_info(row, bed_selectors)
                            if extracted_beds is not None:
                                last_known_bed_info = extracted_beds

                            # B) Detekcia kapacity aktuálneho riadku
                            extracted_occ = self._extract_occupancy(
                                row, occupancy_selectors, occupancy_icon_selectors
                            )
                            if extracted_occ is not None:
                                last_known_occupancy = extracted_occ

                            # Ak riadok explicitne patrí inej kapacite než požadovanej, preskakujeme
                            if last_known_occupancy != requested_guests:
                                continue

                            # C) Extrakcia ceny
                            price: Optional[float] = None
                            for p_sel in price_selectors:
                                p_loc = row.locator(p_sel).first
                                if p_loc.count() > 0 and p_loc.is_visible():
                                    raw_p = p_loc.text_content()
                                    if raw_p:
                                        p_val = clean_price_text(raw_p, price_regex=price_regex)
                                        if p_val is not None and p_val > 0:
                                            price = p_val
                                            break

                            if price is None:
                                continue

                            # D) Strava a storno podmienky
                            meal_plan: Optional[str] = None
                            for m_sel in meal_plan_selectors:
                                m_loc = row.locator(m_sel).first
                                if m_loc.count() > 0:
                                    raw_m = m_loc.text_content()
                                    if raw_m and raw_m.strip():
                                        meal_plan = " ".join(raw_m.split())
                                        break

                            cancellation: Optional[str] = None
                            for c_sel in cancellation_selectors:
                                c_loc = row.locator(c_sel).first
                                if c_loc.count() > 0:
                                    practical_c = c_loc.text_content()
                                    if practical_c and practical_c.strip():
                                        cancellation = " ".join(practical_c.split())
                                        break

                            matched_offers.append(
                                RoomOffer(
                                    room_name=current_room_name,
                                    occupancy=requested_guests,
                                    price=price,
                                    currency="EUR",
                                    meal_plan=meal_plan,
                                    cancellation_policy=cancellation,
                                    bed_info=last_known_bed_info,
                                )
                            )

                        except Exception as row_exc:
                            logger.debug(f"Chyba pri spracovaní riadku {idx} pre {hotel_name}: {row_exc}")
                            continue

                    if matched_offers:
                        break

                except Exception as sel_exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Chyba pri selektore riadkov '{row_selector}': {sel_exc}")
                    continue

            if matched_offers:
                lowest_price = min(o.price for o in matched_offers)
                logger.info(
                    f"Úspešne vyťažených {len(matched_offers)} ponúk pre {hotel_name} "
                    f"(počet hostí: {requested_guests}, najnižšia cena: {lowest_price:.2f} €)"
                )
                return ScrapedResult(
                    hotel_name=hotel_name,
                    is_available=True,
                    booking_price=lowest_price,
                    offers=matched_offers,
                    error_message=None,
                )

            # Fallback pre zobrazenie bez tabuľky riadkov (iba ak nebola detegovaná tabuľka ponúk)
            if not rows_detected_on_page:
                for selector in price_selectors:
                    try:
                        locator = page.locator(selector).first
                        if locator.count() > 0 and locator.is_visible():
                            raw_text = locator.text_content()
                            if raw_text:
                                price = clean_price_text(raw_text, price_regex=price_regex)
                                if price is not None and price > 0:
                                    logger.info(
                                        f"Nájdená cena pre {hotel_name} cez fallback selektor '{selector}': {price:.2f} €"
                                    )
                                    single_offer = RoomOffer(
                                        room_name="Štandardná ponuka",
                                        occupancy=requested_guests,
                                        price=price,
                                        currency="EUR",
                                        meal_plan=None,
                                        cancellation_policy=None,
                                    )
                                    return ScrapedResult(
                                        hotel_name=hotel_name,
                                        is_available=True,
                                        booking_price=price,
                                        offers=[single_offer],
                                        error_message=None,
                                    )
                    except Exception as exc:
                        logger.debug(f"Chyba pri fallback price_selector '{selector}': {exc}")

            # Diagnostický screenshot ak cena ani nedostupnosť neboli nájdené
            self._save_diagnostic_screenshot(page, hotel_name)
            err_msg = f"Cena ani stav nedostupnosti neboli identifikované (počet hostí: {requested_guests})"
            logger.warning(f"Pre hotel '{hotel_name}': {err_msg}")
            return ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                is_available=False,
                booking_price=None,
                offers=[],
                error_message=err_msg,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"Kritická chyba pri parsovaní Booking stránky pre {hotel_name}: {exc}")
            self._save_diagnostic_screenshot(page, hotel_name)
            return ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                is_available=False,
                booking_price=None,
                offers=[],
                error_message=f"Chyba parsovania: {exc}",
            )

==================== FILE: ./scraper/browser.py ====================

"""Playwright browser lifecycle and anti-bot stealth manager."""

import random
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from playwright.sync_api import Browser, BrowserContext, Page, Playwright, sync_playwright
from playwright_stealth import Stealth

from config.settings import get_settings
from core.logger import logger


class BrowserInitError(Exception):
    """Raised when browser initialization or context creation fails."""
    pass


class BrowserManager:
    """Manages stealth-enabled Chromium browser instances and contexts."""

    # Default browser flags to bypass bot detection heuristics
    DEFAULT_CHROMIUM_ARGS = [
        "--disable-blink-features=AutomationControlled",
        "--no-sandbox",
        "--disable-infobars",
        "--disable-dev-shm-usage",
        "--disable-extensions",
        "--disable-notifications",
        "--no-first-run",
        "--ignore-certificate-errors",
    ]

    def __init__(
        self,
        headless: bool = True,
        timeout_ms: Optional[int] = None,
        user_agents: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        viewport: Optional[Dict[str, int]] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize BrowserManager settings.

        Args:
            headless: Whether to run Chromium in headless mode.
            timeout_ms: Default timeout for context and page actions.
            user_agents: List of desktop User-Agents to pick from.
            viewport: Viewport dictionary with 'width' and 'height'.
        """
        settings = get_settings()
        self.headless = headless
        self.timeout_ms = timeout_ms if timeout_ms is not None else settings.default_timeout_ms
        self.user_agents = user_agents or settings.user_agents
        self.viewport = viewport or {"width": 1920, "height": 1080}

        self._playwright: Optional[Playwright] = None
        self._browser: Optional[Browser] = None
        self._context: Optional[BrowserContext] = None
        self._stealth = Stealth()

    @property
    def context(self) -> Optional[BrowserContext]:
        """Return the active BrowserContext if launched."""
        return self._context

    @property
    def is_running(self) -> bool:
        """Check if browser process is actively running."""
        return self._browser is not None and self._browser.is_connected()

    def launch_browser(self, headless: Optional[bool] = None) -> BrowserContext:
        """Launch Chromium with stealth configurations and create a BrowserContext.

        Args:
            headless: Override instance headless configuration if specified.

        Returns:
            Initialized and stealth-patched BrowserContext.

        Raises:
            BrowserInitError: If browser launch or context creation fails.
        """
        run_headless = self.headless if headless is None else headless
        selected_ua = random.choice(self.user_agents) if self.user_agents else None

        try:
            logger.debug("Starting Playwright driver...")
            self._playwright = sync_playwright().start()

            logger.debug(
                f"Launching Chromium (headless={run_headless}) with stealth flags..."
            )
            self._browser = self._playwright.chromium.launch(
                headless=run_headless,
                args=self.DEFAULT_CHROMIUM_ARGS,
            )

            context_kwargs: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "viewport": self.viewport,
                "locale": "en-US",
                "timezone_id": "Europe/Bratislava",
                "device_scale_factor": 1.0,
                "has_touch": False,
                "is_mobile": False,
            }
            if selected_ua:
                context_kwargs["user_agent"] = selected_ua

            logger.debug(f"Creating browser context with UA: {selected_ua}...")
            self._context = self._browser.new_context(**context_kwargs)

            # Set default timeout for all operations in this context
            self._context.set_default_timeout(self.timeout_ms)
            self._context.set_default_navigation_timeout(self.timeout_ms)

            # Apply stealth evasion scripts to context
            self._stealth.apply_stealth_sync(self._context)

            logger.info("Chromium stealth browser context initialized successfully.")
            return self._context

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.critical(f"Failed to initialize Playwright browser: {exc}")
            self.close()
            raise BrowserInitError(f"Failed to initialize Playwright browser: {exc}") from exc

    def new_page(self) -> Page:
        """Create a new page in the active context and apply stealth evasions.

        Returns:
            Stealth-enabled Playwright Page.

        Raises:
            BrowserInitError: If browser context is not active.
        """
        if self._context is None:
            self.launch_browser()

        assert self._context is not None
        page = self._context.new_page()
        self._stealth.apply_stealth_sync(page)
        return page

    def close(self) -> None:
        """Safely close context, browser, and Playwright driver without leaking processes."""
        try:
            if self._context is not None:
                try:
                    self._context.close()
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Error closing browser context: {exc}")
                self._context = None

            if self._browser is not None:
                try:
                    self._browser.close()
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Error closing browser: {exc}")
                self._browser = None

            if self._playwright is not None:
                try:
                    self._playwright.stop()
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Error stopping Playwright: {exc}")
                self._playwright = None

            logger.debug("BrowserManager resources released cleanly.")
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(f"Unexpected error while closing browser resources: {exc}")

    def __enter__(self) -> "BrowserManager":
        """Enter context manager."""
        self.launch_browser()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type: Any, exc_val: Any, exc_tb: Any) -> None:
        """Exit context manager ensuring resource release."""
        self.close()


==================== FILE: ./scraper/direct_parser.py ====================

"""Dynamic direct hotel booking engine parser adhering to Zero-Update architecture."""

from datetime import datetime
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
from urllib.parse import parse_qsl, urlencode, urlparse, urlunparse

from playwright.sync_api import Locator, Page

from config.settings import get_settings
from core.logger import logger
from core.models import RoomOffer, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from scraper.booking_parser import clean_price_text

SCREENSHOTS_DIR = get_settings().logs_dir / "screenshots"


class DirectParser:
    """Dynamic parser for direct hotel booking systems (Token-based POST, Profitroom, custom)."""

    def __init__(self, screenshots_dir: Optional[Path] = None) -> None:
        self.screenshots_dir = Path(screenshots_dir or SCREENSHOTS_DIR)
        self.screenshots_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def _save_diagnostic_screenshot(self, page: Page, hotel_name: str) -> Optional[Path]:
        """Safely capture a screenshot for debugging unidentified layouts."""
        try:
            safe_slug = re.sub(r"[^\w]+", "_", hotel_name.strip().lower()).strip("_")
            timestamp = datetime.utcnow().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
            filename = f"direct_{safe_slug}_{timestamp}.png"
            target_path = self.screenshots_dir / filename
            page.screenshot(path=str(target_path), full_page=False)
            logger.info(f"Saved direct booking diagnostic screenshot to: {target_path}")
            return target_path
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(f"Could not save diagnostic screenshot for {hotel_name}: {exc}")
            return None

    def build_direct_url(
        self,
        base_url: str,
        job: ScrapingJob,
        rules: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    ) -> str:
        """Construct initial landing URL based on engine method."""
        if not base_url:
            return ""

        effective_rules = (rules.get("rules") if isinstance(rules.get("rules"), dict) else rules) or {}
        method = effective_rules.get("method")

        if method in ("token_api_post", "post_form"):
            return base_url

        date_format = effective_rules.get("date_format", "%Y-%m-%d")
        check_in_str = job.check_in.strftime(date_format)
        check_out_str = job.check_out.strftime(date_format)

        url_pattern: Optional[str] = effective_rules.get("url_pattern") or effective_rules.get("url_template")
        if url_pattern:
            return url_pattern.format(
                base_url=base_url.rstrip("/"),
                check_in=check_in_str,
                check_out=check_out_str,
                adults=job.adults,
                children=job.children,
                total_guests=job.total_guests,
            )

        url_params_mapping: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = effective_rules.get("url_params")
        if url_params_mapping:
            parsed = urlparse(base_url)
            current_params = dict(parse_qsl(parsed.query))
            mapping_replacements = {
                "{check_in}": check_in_str,
                "{check_out}": check_out_str,
                "{adults}": str(job.adults),
                "{children}": str(job.children),
                "{total_guests}": str(job.total_guests),
            }
            for param_key, param_value in url_params_mapping.items():
                val_str = str(param_value)
                for placeholder, actual in mapping_replacements.items():
                    val_str = val_str.replace(placeholder, actual)
                current_params[param_key] = val_str

            return urlunparse(parsed._replace(query=urlencode(current_params)))

        return base_url

    def _dismiss_cookie_banners(self, page: Page, rules: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Dynamically dismiss cookie consent modals based on remote rules."""
        selectors: List[str] = rules.get(
            "cookie_consent_selectors",
            [
                "#onetrust-accept-btn-handler",
                "button[id*='cookie'][id*='accept']",
                "button:has-text('Prijať všetky')",
                "button:has-text('Súhlasím')",
                ".cookie-bar button",
            ],
        )
        for selector in selectors:
            try:
                locator = page.locator(selector).first
                if locator.count() > 0 and locator.is_visible(timeout=1500):
                    locator.click(timeout=1500)
                    logger.debug(f"Direct cookie banner dismissed via selector: {selector}")
                    page.wait_for_timeout(500)
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue

    def _handle_token_api_post(self, page: Page, rules: Dict[str, Any], job: ScrapingJob) -> bool:
        """Fetch search token from API endpoint and navigate directly to search results."""
        token_endpoint = rules.get("token_endpoint", "https://booking.hotelpermon.sk/sk/home/createSearchToken")
        search_template = rules.get("search_url_template", "https://booking.hotelpermon.sk/sk/search/{token}")
        raw_payload = rules.get("form_payload", {})
        date_format = rules.get("date_format", "%Y-%m-%d")
        child_age = str(job.children_ages[0]) if (hasattr(job, "children_ages") and job.children_ages) else "6"

        replacements = {
            "{check_in}": job.check_in.strftime(date_format),
            "{check_out}": job.check_out.strftime(date_format),
            "{adults}": str(job.adults),
            "{children}": str(job.children),
            "{total_guests}": str(job.total_guests),
            "{child_age_1}": child_age,
        }

        resolved_payload: Dict[str, str] = {}
        for k, v in raw_payload.items():
            val = str(v)
            for placeholder, actual in replacements.items():
                val = val.replace(placeholder, actual)
            resolved_payload[k] = val

        logger.info(f"Generujem search token cez POST na {token_endpoint}...")

        # Volanie fetch priamo v kontexte stránky so správnymi cookies a headers
        response_text = page.evaluate(
            """async ({ endpoint, payload }) => {
                const formBody = new URLSearchParams(payload).toString();
                const res = await fetch(endpoint, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded; charset=UTF-8',
                        'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                    },
                    body: formBody
                });
                return await res.text();
            }""",
            {"endpoint": token_endpoint, "payload": resolved_payload},
        )

        logger.debug(f"Odpoveď token API: {response_text}")

        # Extrakcia tokenu (môže to byť UUID reťazec alebo JSON)
        token = ""
        try:
            data = json.loads(response_text)
            if isinstance(data, dict):
                token = str(data.get("token") or data.get("id") or data.get("search_token") or "")
            elif isinstance(data, str):
                token = data
        except Exception:
            token = response_text.strip().strip('"')

        # Regex vyhľadanie UUID tokenu ak je odpoveď komplexnejšia
        if not token or len(token) > 100:
            match = re.search(r"([a-f0-9]{8}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{4}-[a-f0-9]{12})", response_text)
            if match:
                token = match.group(1)

        if not token:
            logger.warning(f"Nepodarilo sa získať search token z odpovede: {response_text[:200]}")
            return False

        target_url = search_template.replace("{token}", token)
        logger.info(f"Získaný search token: {token} -> Navigujem na výsledky: {target_url}")
        try:
            # 'commit' znamená: ako náhle server odpovie hlavičkami a začne tiecť HTML, pokračuj ďalej
            page.goto(target_url, wait_until="commit", timeout=15000)
        except Exception as nav_exc:
            logger.debug(f"Navigácia na token URL hlási stav: {nav_exc}")

        return True

    def parse_room_catalog(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
    ) -> List[RoomOffer]:
        """Parse static direct hotel catalog page (without dates or token POST) for room names."""
        effective_rules = (rules.get("rules") if isinstance(rules.get("rules"), dict) else rules) or {}
        try:
            try:
                page.wait_for_load_state("domcontentloaded", timeout=10000)
            except Exception:
                pass

            self._dismiss_cookie_banners(page, effective_rules)

            wait_sel = effective_rules.get("catalog_wait_for_selector") or effective_rules.get(
                "wait_for_selector"
            )
            if wait_sel:
                try:
                    page.wait_for_selector(wait_sel, timeout=8000)
                except Exception:
                    page.wait_for_timeout(1500)
            else:
                page.wait_for_timeout(500)

            catalog_selectors: List[str] = (
                effective_rules.get("catalog_room_selectors")
                or effective_rules.get("room_name_selectors")
                or [
                    ".room-title",
                    ".room-name",
                    "h2.room-heading",
                    "h3.room-title",
                    ".room-card h2",
                    ".room-card h3",
                    ".room-item h2",
                    ".room-item h3",
                ]
            )

            offers: List[RoomOffer] = []
            seen_names = set()

            targets: List[Any] = [page]
            for frame in page.frames:
                if frame != page.main_frame:
                    targets.append(frame)

            for target in targets:
                for selector in catalog_selectors:
                    try:
                        loc = target.locator(selector)
                        count = loc.count()
                        for i in range(count):
                            el = loc.nth(i)
                            raw_text = el.text_content()
                            if not raw_text:
                                continue
                            cleaned = " ".join(raw_text.split())
                            if not cleaned or len(cleaned) > 120:
                                continue
                            norm = cleaned.lower()
                            if norm not in seen_names:
                                seen_names.add(norm)
                                offers.append(RoomOffer(room_name=cleaned, price=0.0))
                    except Exception as exc:
                        logger.debug(
                            f"Chyba pri katalógovom selektore '{selector}' pre {hotel_name}: {exc}"
                        )
                        continue

            logger.info(
                f"Katalóg priameho webu pre '{hotel_name}': nájdených {len(offers)} unikátnych izieb."
            )
            return offers
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"Chyba pri parsovaní priameho katalógu pre {hotel_name}: {exc}")
            return []

    def _extract_direct_offers(
        self,
        target: Any,
        effective_rules: Dict[str, Any],
        requested_guests: int,
    ) -> List[RoomOffer]:
        """Extract room-level offers from room cards using dynamic rules."""
        room_card_selectors: List[str] = effective_rules.get("room_card_selectors", [])
        if not room_card_selectors:
            return []

        room_name_selectors: List[str] = effective_rules.get(
            "room_name_selectors",
            [".room-title", ".room-name", "h2", "h3", "h4"],
        )
        price_selectors: List[str] = effective_rules.get("price_selectors", [])
        price_regex: Optional[str] = effective_rules.get("price_regex")
        if not price_selectors:
            return []

        extracted_offers: List[RoomOffer] = []
        for card_sel in room_card_selectors:
            try:
                cards = target.locator(card_sel)
                card_count = cards.count()
                if card_count == 0:
                    continue

                for idx in range(card_count):
                    card = cards.nth(idx)
                    room_name: Optional[str] = None
                    for n_sel in room_name_selectors:
                        try:
                            n_loc = card.locator(n_sel).first
                            if n_loc.count() > 0:
                                raw_name = n_loc.text_content()
                                if raw_name:
                                    cleaned_name = " ".join(raw_name.split())
                                    if cleaned_name and len(cleaned_name) <= 120:
                                        room_name = cleaned_name
                                        break
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                    if not room_name:
                        continue

                    card_prices: List[float] = []
                    for p_sel in price_selectors:
                        try:
                            p_locs = card.locator(p_sel)
                            p_count = p_locs.count()
                            for p_idx in range(p_count):
                                p_el = p_locs.nth(p_idx)
                                if p_el.is_visible():
                                    raw_p = p_el.text_content()
                                    if not raw_p or len(raw_p.strip()) > 40:
                                        continue
                                    p_val = clean_price_text(raw_p, price_regex=price_regex)
                                    if p_val is not None and p_val >= 20.0:
                                        card_prices.append(p_val)
                        except Exception:
                            continue

                    if card_prices:
                        extracted_offers.append(
                            RoomOffer(
                                room_name=room_name,
                                occupancy=requested_guests,
                                price=min(card_prices),
                                currency="EUR",
                            )
                        )

                if extracted_offers:
                    break
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.debug(f"Chyba pri parsovaní karty izby '{card_sel}': {exc}")
                continue

        return extracted_offers

    def parse_direct_hotel(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
        direct_url: Optional[str] = None,
        job: Optional[ScrapingJob] = None,
    ) -> ScrapedResult:
        """Entrypoint for direct hotel parsing."""
        return self._parse_direct_page_internal(
            page=page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name=hotel_name,
            direct_url=direct_url,
            job=job,
        )

    def parse_direct_page(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
        direct_url: Optional[str] = None,
        job: Optional[ScrapingJob] = None,
    ) -> ScrapedResult:
        """Alias matching parse_direct_hotel."""
        return self._parse_direct_page_internal(
            page=page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name=hotel_name,
            direct_url=direct_url,
            job=job,
        )

    def _parse_direct_page_internal(
        self,
        page: Page,
        rules: Dict[str, Any],
        hotel_name: str,
        direct_url: Optional[str] = None,
        job: Optional[ScrapingJob] = None,
    ) -> ScrapedResult:
        """Defensively parse direct page with dynamic rules and form interaction."""
        effective_rules = rules.get("rules") if isinstance(rules.get("rules"), dict) else rules
        method = effective_rules.get("method")

        logger.info(
            f"DirectParser spustený pre '{hotel_name}': method='{method}', "
            f"job={'OK' if job is not None else 'None'}"
        )

        try:
            try:
                page.wait_for_load_state("domcontentloaded", timeout=10000)
            except Exception:
                pass

            self._dismiss_cookie_banners(page, effective_rules)

            # 1. Ak systém vyžaduje token cez AJAX POST
            # 1. Vygenerovanie tokenu a prechod na výsledky
            if method == "token_api_post":
                if job is None:
                    logger.error(
                        f"KRITICKÁ CHYBA: Engine pre '{hotel_name}' je 'token_api_post', "
                        f"ale parameter 'job' prišiel ako None! Skontroluj volanie v engine.py."
                    )
                else:
                    self._handle_token_api_post(page, effective_rules, job)  

            # 2. Defenzívne čakanie na výsledkové elementy
            wait_sel = effective_rules.get("wait_for_selector")
            if wait_sel:
                try:
                    page.wait_for_selector(wait_sel, timeout=10000)
                except Exception:
                    page.wait_for_timeout(4000)
            else:
                page.wait_for_timeout(3000)

            self._dismiss_cookie_banners(page, effective_rules)

            # 3. Kontrola vypredania
            sold_out_selectors: List[str] = effective_rules.get("sold_out_selectors", [])
            for selector in sold_out_selectors:
                try:
                    locator = page.locator(selector)
                    count = locator.count()
                    if count > 0 and any(locator.nth(i).is_visible() for i in range(count)):
                        logger.info(f"Hotel {hotel_name} je na priamom webe vypredaný.")
                        return ScrapedResult(
                            hotel_name=hotel_name,
                            direct_price=None,
                            direct_url=page.url,
                            is_available=False,
                            error_message=None,
                        )
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Error checking sold_out_selector '{selector}': {exc}")

            # 4. Extrakcia cien a izieb
            price_selectors: List[str] = effective_rules.get("price_selectors", [])
            price_regex: Optional[str] = effective_rules.get("price_regex")
            requested_guests = job.adults if job is not None else 2
            extracted_prices: List[float] = []

            direct_offers: List[RoomOffer] = self._extract_direct_offers(
                page, effective_rules, requested_guests
            )
            for frame in page.frames:
                if frame == page.main_frame:
                    continue
                direct_offers.extend(
                    self._extract_direct_offers(frame, effective_rules, requested_guests)
                )
            for offer in direct_offers:
                if offer.price >= 20.0:
                    extracted_prices.append(offer.price)

            # Hlavný rámec
            for selector in price_selectors:
                try:
                    locator = page.locator(selector)
                    count = locator.count()
                    for i in range(count):
                        el = locator.nth(i)
                        if el.is_visible():
                            raw_text = el.text_content()
                            # Filter: ignoruj obrovské bloky (kalendáre, celé karty, podmienky)
                            if not raw_text or len(raw_text.strip()) > 40:
                                continue

                            p_val = clean_price_text(raw_text, price_regex=price_regex)
                            if p_val is not None and p_val >= 20.0:
                                extracted_prices.append(p_val)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.debug(f"Chyba pri extrakcii ceny ({selector}): {exc}")

            # Vnorené iFramy
            for frame in page.frames:
                if frame == page.main_frame:
                    continue
                for selector in price_selectors:
                    try:
                        loc = frame.locator(selector)
                        count = loc.count()
                        for i in range(count):
                            el = loc.nth(i)
                            if el.is_visible():
                                raw_text = el.text_content()
                                if not raw_text or len(raw_text.strip()) > 40:
                                    continue

                                p_val = clean_price_text(raw_text, price_regex=price_regex)
                                if p_val is not None and p_val >= 20.0:
                                    extracted_prices.append(p_val)
                    except Exception:
                        continue

            if extracted_prices:
                final_direct_price = min(extracted_prices)
                logger.info(
                    f"Úspešne extrahovaná priama cena pre {hotel_name}: {final_direct_price:.2f} EUR "
                    f"(zachytených hodnôt: {len(extracted_prices)}, izieb: {len(direct_offers)}, URL: {page.url})"
                )
                return ScrapedResult(
                    hotel_name=hotel_name,
                    direct_price=final_direct_price,
                    direct_url=page.url,
                    is_available=True,
                    direct_offers=direct_offers,
                    error_message=None,
                )

            # Neúspešná identifikácia
            warning_msg = (
                f"Cena ani stav nedostupnosti neboli identifikované na priamom webe: {hotel_name} "
                f"(aktuálna URL: {page.url})"
            )
            logger.warning(warning_msg)
            self._save_diagnostic_screenshot(page, hotel_name)

            return ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                direct_price=None,
                direct_url=page.url,
                is_available=False,
                error_message="Cena ani stav nedostupnosti na priamom webe neboli identifikované",
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error(f"Neočakávaná chyba pri parsovaní priameho webu ({hotel_name}): {exc}")
            self._save_diagnostic_screenshot(page, hotel_name)
            return ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                direct_price=None,
                direct_url=page.url,
                is_available=False,
                error_message=str(exc),
            )

==================== FILE: ./scraper/engine.py ====================

"""Main scraping orchestrator engine executing hotel price comparisons."""

from datetime import date, timedelta
import queue
import random
import time
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from playwright.sync_api import Page

from config.settings import get_settings
from core.config_manager import ConfigError, ConfigManager
from core.logger import logger
from core.models import ResultStatus, RoomOffer, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from core.room_matcher import RoomMatcher
from scraper.booking_parser import BookingParser
from scraper.browser import BrowserManager
from scraper.direct_parser import DirectParser


class ScrapingEngine:
    """Orchestrates end-to-end hotel price scraping across Booking.com and direct websites."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        job: Optional[ScrapingJob] = None,
        config_manager: Optional[ConfigManager] = None,
        progress_queue: Optional[Any] = None,
        browser_manager: Optional[BrowserManager] = None,
        min_jitter_sec: Optional[float] = None,
        max_jitter_sec: Optional[float] = None,
        stop_event: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize ScrapingEngine.

        Args:
            job: Optional ScrapingJob defining stay dates, guests, and target hotels.
            config_manager: Injected ConfigManager for remote rules retrieval.
            progress_queue: Optional queue.Queue or QueueManager for UI communication.
            browser_manager: Optional custom or mock BrowserManager instance.
            min_jitter_sec: Minimum stealth pause between hotel requests.
            max_jitter_sec: Maximum stealth pause between hotel requests.
            stop_event: Optional threading.Event to abort execution gracefully.
        """
        settings = get_settings()

        if job is None:
            today = date.today()
            job = ScrapingJob(check_in=today, check_out=today + timedelta(days=1))

        self.job = job
        self.config_manager = config_manager or ConfigManager()
        self.progress_queue = progress_queue
        self.browser_manager = browser_manager or BrowserManager()
        self.min_jitter = (
            min_jitter_sec if min_jitter_sec is not None else settings.min_jitter_sec
        )
        self.max_jitter = (
            max_jitter_sec if max_jitter_sec is not None else settings.max_jitter_sec
        )
        self.stop_event = stop_event

        self.booking_parser = BookingParser()
        self.direct_parser = DirectParser()
        self.room_matcher = RoomMatcher()
        self._rules_cache: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}

    def _send_progress(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Safely deliver progress notifications to the UI queue."""
        if self.progress_queue is None:
            return
        try:
            if hasattr(self.progress_queue, "put"):
                self.progress_queue.put(payload)
            elif hasattr(self.progress_queue, "publish"):
                self.progress_queue.publish(payload)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(f"Failed to post to progress_queue: {exc}")

    def _get_rules(self, engine_name: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Fetch and cache scraping rules for the specified engine."""
        if engine_name not in self._rules_cache:
            try:
                self._rules_cache[engine_name] = self.config_manager.get_scraping_rules(
                    engine_name
                )
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    f"Could not load scraping rules for engine '{engine_name}': {exc}"
                )
                self._rules_cache[engine_name] = {}
        return self._rules_cache[engine_name]

    def _scrape_single_hotel(
        self,
        hotel: Dict[str, Any],
        page: Page,
        booking_rules: Dict[str, Any],
    ) -> ScrapedResult:
        """Scrape Booking.com and direct website for one hotel in isolated try-except."""
        hotel_id = str(hotel.get("id", ""))
        hotel_name = str(hotel.get("name", "Unknown Hotel"))
        base_booking_url = hotel.get("booking_url", "")
        base_direct_url = hotel.get("direct_url", "")
        direct_engine = hotel.get("direct_booking_engine", "generic")

        resolved_booking_url = ""
        resolved_direct_url = ""
        booking_result: Optional[ScrapedResult] = None
        direct_result: Optional[ScrapedResult] = None
        error_messages: List[str] = []

        # 1. Scrape Booking.com
        if base_booking_url:
            try:
                resolved_booking_url = self.booking_parser.build_booking_url(
                    base_booking_url, self.job
                )
                logger.debug(f"Navigating to Booking: {resolved_booking_url}")
                page.goto(resolved_booking_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded")
                booking_result = self.booking_parser.parse_hotel_page(
                    page=page,
                    rules=booking_rules,
                    hotel_name=hotel_name,
                    requested_guests=self.job.adults,
                )
                if booking_result.error_message:
                    error_messages.append(f"Booking: {booking_result.error_message}")
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error(f"Error scraping Booking.com for {hotel_name}: {exc}")
                error_messages.append(f"Booking error: {exc}")

        # 2. Scrape Direct Website
        if base_direct_url:
            try:
                direct_rules = self._get_rules(direct_engine)
                logger.info(
                    f"Spúšťam Direct pre '{hotel_name}' s job: {self.job.check_in} -> {self.job.check_out}"
                )

                resolved_direct_url = self.direct_parser.build_direct_url(
                    base_direct_url, self.job, direct_rules
                )
                logger.debug(f"Navigating to Direct: {resolved_direct_url}")
                page.goto(resolved_direct_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded")

                direct_result = self.direct_parser.parse_direct_hotel(
                    page=page,
                    rules=direct_rules,
                    hotel_name=hotel_name,
                    direct_url=resolved_direct_url,
                    job=self.job,
                )
                if direct_result.error_message:
                    error_messages.append(f"Direct: {direct_result.error_message}")
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.error(f"Error scraping direct site for {hotel_name}: {exc}")
                error_messages.append(f"Direct error: {exc}")

        # 3. Consolidate into merged ScrapedResult & apply saved room mappings
        b_price = booking_result.booking_price if booking_result else None
        d_price = direct_result.direct_price if direct_result else None
        all_offers = list(booking_result.offers) if booking_result else []
        direct_offers = list(direct_result.direct_offers) if direct_result else []

        try:
            saved_mappings = self.config_manager.get_room_mappings(hotel_id)
            if isinstance(saved_mappings, list) and saved_mappings:
                all_offers, direct_offers = self.room_matcher.apply_saved_mappings(
                    all_offers, direct_offers, saved_mappings
                )
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.warning(f"Failed to apply saved room mappings for '{hotel_name}': {exc}")

        currency = (
            (booking_result.currency if booking_result else None)
            or (direct_result.currency if direct_result else None)
            or "EUR"
        )

        # Availability logic
        is_avail = False
        if booking_result and booking_result.is_available:
            is_avail = True
        elif direct_result and direct_result.is_available:
            is_avail = True

        # Status determination
        if b_price is not None and d_price is not None:
            status = ResultStatus.SUCCESS
        elif b_price is not None or d_price is not None:
            status = ResultStatus.PARTIAL
        elif not is_avail and (
            (booking_result and not booking_result.is_available)
            or (direct_result and not direct_result.is_available)
        ):
            status = ResultStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE
        else:
            status = ResultStatus.FAILED

        combined_error = "; ".join(error_messages) if error_messages else None

        return ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id=hotel_id,
            hotel_name=hotel_name,
            check_in=self.job.check_in,
            check_out=self.job.check_out,
            booking_price=b_price,
            direct_price=d_price,
            currency=currency,
            booking_url=resolved_booking_url or base_booking_url,
            direct_url=resolved_direct_url or base_direct_url,
            status=status,
            is_available=is_avail,
            offers=all_offers,
            direct_offers=direct_offers,
            error_message=combined_error,
        )

    def sync_room_catalog(self, hotel_identifiers: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
        """Synchronize date-independent room catalogs and persist mappings to hs-room_mappings."""
        logger.info("Starting date-independent room catalog synchronization (--sync-rooms)...")

        try:
            target_hotels = self.config_manager.get_target_hotels()
        except ConfigError as exc:
            logger.error(f"Cannot synchronize room catalogs without target hotels: {exc}")
            return 0

        filter_ids = hotel_identifiers if hotel_identifiers is not None else self.job.hotel_identifiers
        if filter_ids:
            target_hotels = [
                h
                for h in target_hotels
                if h.get("id") in filter_ids or h.get("name") in filter_ids
            ]

        total = len(target_hotels)
        if total == 0:
            logger.warning("No target hotels matched for room catalog synchronization.")
            return 0

        booking_rules = self._get_rules("booking")
        global_matching_rules = self._get_rules("room_matching")
        total_synced = 0

        try:
            self.browser_manager.launch_browser()

            for index, hotel in enumerate(target_hotels, start=1):
                if self.stop_event is not None and self.stop_event.is_set():
                    logger.info("Room catalog sync aborted due to stop_event signal.")
                    break

                hotel_id = str(hotel.get("id", ""))
                hotel_name = str(hotel.get("name", f"Hotel #{index}"))
                catalog_booking_url = str(
                    hotel.get("booking_rooms_url") or hotel.get("booking_url") or ""
                )
                catalog_direct_url = str(
                    hotel.get("rooms_catalog_url") or hotel.get("direct_url") or ""
                )
                direct_engine = str(hotel.get("direct_booking_engine") or "generic")

                logger.info(f"[{index}/{total}] Syncing room catalog for: {hotel_name}")

                page = self.browser_manager.new_page()
                try:
                    booking_offers: List[RoomOffer] = []
                    direct_offers: List[RoomOffer] = []

                    if catalog_booking_url:
                        try:
                            catalog_booking_url = self.booking_parser.build_catalog_booking_url(
                                catalog_booking_url
                            )
                            logger.debug(f"Navigating to undated Booking catalog: {catalog_booking_url}")
                            page.goto(catalog_booking_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded")
                            booking_offers = self.booking_parser.parse_room_catalog(
                                page=page,
                                rules=booking_rules,
                                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                            )
                        except Exception as exc:
                            logger.error(f"Error syncing Booking room catalog for {hotel_name}: {exc}")

                    if catalog_direct_url:
                        try:
                            direct_rules = self._get_rules(direct_engine)
                            logger.debug(f"Navigating to static direct catalog: {catalog_direct_url}")
                            page.goto(catalog_direct_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded")
                            direct_offers = self.direct_parser.parse_room_catalog(
                                page=page,
                                rules=direct_rules,
                                hotel_name=hotel_name,
                            )
                        except Exception as exc:
                            logger.error(f"Error syncing direct room catalog for {hotel_name}: {exc}")

                    existing_mappings: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
                    try:
                        fetched = self.config_manager.get_room_mappings(hotel_id)
                        if isinstance(fetched, list):
                            existing_mappings = fetched
                    except Exception as exc:
                        logger.warning(f"Could not fetch existing mappings for {hotel_name}: {exc}")

                    mappings = self.room_matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
                        hotel=hotel,
                        booking_offers=booking_offers,
                        direct_offers=direct_offers,
                        global_rules=global_matching_rules,
                        existing_mappings=existing_mappings,
                    )

                    if mappings:
                        if self.config_manager.upsert_room_mappings(mappings):
                            total_synced += len(mappings)
                            logger.info(
                                f"Synchronizovaných {len(mappings)} izieb pre hotel '{hotel_name}'."
                            )
                        else:
                            logger.warning(
                                f"Nepodarilo sa uložiť mapovania izieb pre hotel '{hotel_name}'."
                            )
                    else:
                        logger.warning(f"Pre hotel '{hotel_name}' neboli vyťažené žiadne izby na mapovanie.")
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.error(f"Unexpected error during room sync for {hotel_name}: {exc}")
                finally:
                    try:
                        page.close()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                if index < total and self.max_jitter > 0:
                    pause = random.uniform(self.min_jitter, self.max_jitter)
                    logger.debug(f"Applying stealth pause of {pause:.2f}s...")
                    time.sleep(pause)

            logger.info(
                f"Room catalog synchronization finished. Total synced mappings: {total_synced}."
            )
        finally:
            self.browser_manager.close()

        return total_synced

    def run(self) -> List[ScrapedResult]:
        """Execute the end-to-end price comparison scraping job.

        Returns:
            List of ScrapedResult objects containing paired prices and deltas.
        """
        logger.info(
            f"Starting ScrapingEngine for {self.job.nights} nights "
            f"({self.job.check_in} -> {self.job.check_out}) for {self.job.total_guests} guests."
        )

        # 1. Fetch target hotels
        try:
            target_hotels = self.config_manager.get_target_hotels()
        except ConfigError as exc:
            logger.error(f"Cannot run scraper without target hotels: {exc}")
            self._send_progress({"status": "failed", "error": str(exc)})
            return []

        # Filter hotels if requested in job
        if self.job.hotel_identifiers:
            target_hotels = [
                h
                for h in target_hotels
                if h.get("id") in self.job.hotel_identifiers
                or h.get("name") in self.job.hotel_identifiers
            ]

        total = len(target_hotels)
        if total == 0:
            logger.warning("No target hotels to scrape matching the criteria.")
            self._send_progress({"status": "completed", "results_count": 0})
            return []

        # 2. Pre-fetch rules for Booking
        booking_rules = self._get_rules("booking")

        results: List[ScrapedResult] = []

        # 3. Launch browser and run loop
        try:
            self.browser_manager.launch_browser()

            for index, hotel in enumerate(target_hotels, start=1):
                if self.stop_event is not None and self.stop_event.is_set():
                    logger.info("Scraping loop aborted due to stop_event signal.")
                    break

                hotel_id = hotel.get("id", "")
                hotel_name = hotel.get("name", f"Hotel #{index}")
                pct = round((index / total) * 100, 1)

                # Send granular progress update pre UI
                self._send_progress(
                    {
                        "status": "running",
                        "current": index,
                        "total": total,
                        "hotel": hotel_name,
                        "percent": pct,
                    }
                )

                logger.info(f"[{index}/{total}] Processing hotel: {hotel_name}")

                # Čistá stránka (tab) pre každý hotel na izoláciu cookies a stavov
                page = self.browser_manager.new_page()
                try:
                    result = self._scrape_single_hotel(hotel, page, booking_rules)
                    results.append(result)
                except Exception as exc:
                    logger.error(f"Unexpected crash processing {hotel_name}: {exc}")
                    results.append(
                        ScrapedResult(
                            hotel_id=hotel_id,
                            hotel_name=hotel_name,
                            check_in=self.job.check_in,
                            check_out=self.job.check_out,
                            status=ResultStatus.FAILED,
                            is_available=False,
                            error_message=f"Fatal hotel extraction error: {exc}",
                        )
                    )
                finally:
                    try:
                        page.close()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                # Stealth pauza medzi hotelmi
                if index < total and self.max_jitter > 0:
                    pause = random.uniform(self.min_jitter, self.max_jitter)
                    logger.debug(f"Applying stealth pause of {pause:.2f}s...")
                    time.sleep(pause)

            # 4. Final completion event (až po ukončení celej slučky)
            self._send_progress(
                {
                    "status": "completed",
                    "results_count": len(results),
                    "total": total,
                    "current": total,
                    "percent": 100.0,
                }
            )
            logger.info(f"Scraping job finished. Successfully processed {len(results)} hotels.")

        finally:
            # 5. Always close browser cleanly
            self.browser_manager.close()

        return results
