        self.assertEqual(mappings[1]["direct_room_pattern"], "Suite")

    @patch("requests.post")
    @patch("requests.get")
    def test_upsert_room_mappings_protects_manual_override(
        self, mock_get: MagicMock, mock_post: MagicMock
    ) -> None:
        """Upsert must preserve booking_room_name and capacity_group when is_manual_override=True."""
        existing_db = [
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "suite",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Komfort",
                "booking_room_name": "Manual Executive Suite",
                "is_exclusive_direct": False,
                "is_manual_override": True,
                "match_score": 100,
                "priority": 1,
                "is_active": True,
            }
        ]
        mock_get_resp = MagicMock()
        mock_get_resp.status_code = 200
        mock_get_resp.json.return_value = existing_db
        mock_get.return_value = mock_get_resp

        mock_post_resp = MagicMock()
        mock_post_resp.status_code = 201
        mock_post.return_value = mock_post_resp

        manager = ConfigManager(
            supabase_url=self.fake_url,
            supabase_key=self.fake_key,
            cache_dir=self.cache_dir,
        )

        new_mappings = [
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "2_pax",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Komfort",
                "booking_room_name": "Auto Double Room",
                "is_exclusive_direct": False,
                "is_manual_override": False,
                "match_score": 78,
                "priority": 1,
                "is_active": True,
            },
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "family",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Rodinný apartmán",
                "booking_room_name": None,
                "is_exclusive_direct": True,
                "is_manual_override": False,
                "match_score": 0,
                "priority": 2,
                "is_active": True,
            },
        ]

        ok = manager.upsert_room_mappings(new_mappings)
        self.assertTrue(ok)
        upsert_calls = [
            c for c in mock_post.call_args_list if "hs-room_mappings" in str(c[0][0])
        ]
        self.assertEqual(len(upsert_calls), 1)

        endpoint = upsert_calls[0][0][0]
        kwargs = upsert_calls[0][1]
        self.assertEqual(endpoint, f"{self.fake_url}/rest/v1/hs-room_mappings")
        self.assertEqual(
            kwargs["params"], {"on_conflict": "hotel_id,capacity_group,direct_room_pattern"}
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            kwargs["headers"]["Prefer"],
            "resolution=merge-duplicates,return=representation",
        )

        sent_payload = kwargs["json"]
        self.assertEqual(len(sent_payload), 2)

        # Manual override record must retain manual booking_room_name & capacity_group
        overridden = sent_payload[0]
        self.assertEqual(overridden["booking_room_name"], "Manual Executive Suite")
        self.assertEqual(overridden["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertTrue(overridden["is_manual_override"])
        self.assertIn("last_synced_at", overridden)

        # Non-overridden record goes through normally
        normal = sent_payload[1]
        self.assertEqual(normal["capacity_group"], "family")
        self.assertIsNone(normal["booking_room_name"])

        # Cache file updated
        cache_file = self.cache_dir / "room_mappings_cache.json"
        self.assertTrue(cache_file.exists())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_engine.py ====================

"""Unit and integration tests for ScrapingEngine orchestrator."""

from datetime import date, timedelta
import queue
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from core.models import ResultStatus, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from scraper.engine import ScrapingEngine


class TestScrapingEngine(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for ScrapingEngine execution, progress dispatch, and defensive recovery."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.today = date.today()
        self.check_in = self.today + timedelta(days=2)
        self.check_out = self.today + timedelta(days=5)

        self.job = ScrapingJob(
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            adults=2,
            children=0,
        )

        self.sample_hotels = [
            {
                "id": "hotel_1",
                "name": "Hotel Alpha",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/alpha",
                "direct_url": "https://alpha.com",
                "direct_booking_engine": "hoteladmin",
            },
            {
                "id": "hotel_2",
                "name": "Hotel Beta (Crashing)",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/beta",
                "direct_url": "https://beta.com",
                "direct_booking_engine": "profitroom",
            },
            {
                "id": "hotel_3",
                "name": "Hotel Gamma",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/gamma",
                "direct_url": "https://gamma.com",
                "direct_booking_engine": "hoteladmin",
            },
        ]

    def test_engine_run_with_defensive_recovery_and_queue(self) -> None:
        """Verify that failure in hotel #2 does not stop hotel #3 and progress_queue is populated."""
        progress_q: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()

        # Mock ConfigManager
        mock_config_manager = MagicMock()
        mock_config_manager.get_target_hotels.return_value = self.sample_hotels
        mock_config_manager.get_scraping_rules.return_value = {
            "price_selectors": [".price"],
            "sold_out_selectors": [".sold-out"],
        }

        # Mock BrowserManager and Page
        mock_browser_manager = MagicMock()
        mock_page = MagicMock()
        mock_browser_manager.new_page.return_value = mock_page

        engine = ScrapingEngine(
            job=self.job,
            config_manager=mock_config_manager,
            progress_queue=progress_q,
            browser_manager=mock_browser_manager,
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,  # Zero jitter for fast test execution
        )

        # Mock parser returns for each hotel:
        # Hotel Alpha: Booking 200 EUR, Direct 180 EUR
        # Hotel Beta: Exception / Crash
        # Hotel Gamma: Booking 150 EUR, Direct 150 EUR
        def mock_parse_booking(page, rules, hotel_name, **kwargs):
            if "Beta" in hotel_name:
                raise RuntimeError("DOM detached crash during parse")
            return ScrapedResult(hotel_name=hotel_name, booking_price=200.0 if "Alpha" in hotel_name else 150.0)

        def mock_parse_direct(page, rules, hotel_name, direct_url=None, **kwargs):
            return ScrapedResult(hotel_name=hotel_name, direct_price=180.0 if "Alpha" in hotel_name else 150.0)

        with patch.object(engine.booking_parser, "parse_hotel_page", side_effect=mock_parse_booking), \
             patch.object(engine.direct_parser, "parse_direct_hotel", side_effect=mock_parse_direct):

            results = engine.run()

        # 1. Check all 3 hotels were processed despite crash on hotel #2
        self.assertEqual(len(results), 3)

        # Hotel 1 (Alpha): Success
        res1 = results[0]
        self.assertEqual(res1.hotel_name, "Hotel Alpha")
        self.assertEqual(res1.booking_price, 200.0)
        self.assertEqual(res1.direct_price, 180.0)
        self.assertEqual(res1.price_delta, -20.0)
        self.assertEqual(res1.cheaper_channel, "DIRECT")
        self.assertEqual(res1.status, ResultStatus.SUCCESS)

        # Hotel 2 (Beta): Recovered from crash, partial or error recorded
        res2 = results[1]
        self.assertEqual(res2.hotel_name, "Hotel Beta (Crashing)")
        self.assertIn("DOM detached crash", res2.error_message or "")
        # Since Direct succeeded (150.0), it has direct_price
        self.assertEqual(res2.direct_price, 150.0)
        self.assertIsNone(res2.booking_price)

        # Hotel 3 (Gamma): Succeeded after hotel 2 recovered
        res3 = results[2]
        self.assertEqual(res3.hotel_name, "Hotel Gamma")
        self.assertEqual(res3.booking_price, 150.0)
        self.assertEqual(res3.direct_price, 150.0)
        self.assertEqual(res3.price_delta, 0.0)

        # 2. Verify progress_queue messages
        messages = []
        while not progress_q.empty():
            messages.append(progress_q.get_nowait())

        # Should have 3 "running" updates and 1 "completed"
        running_msgs = [m for m in messages if m.get("status") == "running"]
        completed_msgs = [m for m in messages if m.get("status") == "completed"]

        self.assertEqual(len(running_msgs), 3)
        self.assertEqual(len(completed_msgs), 1)

        self.assertEqual(running_msgs[0]["current"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(running_msgs[0]["total"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(running_msgs[0]["percent"], 33.3)

        self.assertEqual(running_msgs[2]["current"], 3)
        self.assertEqual(running_msgs[2]["percent"], 100.0)

        self.assertEqual(completed_msgs[0]["results_count"], 3)

        # 3. Verify browser was cleanly closed in finally block
        mock_browser_manager.close.assert_called_once()

    def test_hotel_identifiers_filtering(self) -> None:
        """Verify engine filters target hotels when hotel_identifiers are specified in job."""
        filtered_job = ScrapingJob(
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            hotel_identifiers=["hotel_1"],
        )

        mock_config_manager = MagicMock()
        mock_config_manager.get_target_hotels.return_value = self.sample_hotels
        mock_config_manager.get_scraping_rules.return_value = {}

        mock_browser_manager = MagicMock()

        engine = ScrapingEngine(
            job=filtered_job,
            config_manager=mock_config_manager,
            browser_manager=mock_browser_manager,
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,
        )

        with patch.object(engine, "_scrape_single_hotel") as mock_scrape:
            mock_scrape.return_value = ScrapedResult(hotel_name="Hotel Alpha")
            results = engine.run()

        self.assertEqual(len(results), 1)
        self.assertEqual(mock_scrape.call_count, 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

==================== FILE: ./tests/test_excel_generator.py ====================

"""Unit tests for ExcelReportGenerator with Pandas and OpenPyXL."""

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest

import openpyxl
import pandas as pd

from core.models import ResultStatus, RoomOffer, ScrapedResult
from exporters.excel_generator import ExcelReportGenerator, ExportError


class TestExcelReportGenerator(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for ExcelReportGenerator report creation, delta computation, and styling."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.output_file = Path(self.test_dir) / "test_hotel_report.xlsx"
        self.generator = ExcelReportGenerator()

        self.sample_results = [
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="hotel_grand",
                hotel_name="Grand Hotel Kempinski",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 4),
                booking_price=250.0,
                direct_price=220.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
                is_available=True,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="hotel_marrols",
                hotel_name="Boutique Hotel Marrol's",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 4),
                booking_price=150.0,
                direct_price=190.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
                is_available=True,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="hotel_sold_out",
                hotel_name="Carlton Hotel",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 4),
                booking_price=None,
                direct_price=None,
                status=ResultStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE,
                is_available=False,
                error_message="Vypredané na oboch platformách",
            ),
        ]

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        if Path(self.test_dir).exists():
            shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_generate_comparison_report_file_and_deltas(self) -> None:
        """Verify report creation, overview columns, and delta calculations."""
        ret_path = self.generator.generate_comparison_report(
            results=self.sample_results,
            output_path=self.output_file,
        )

        self.assertEqual(ret_path, str(self.output_file.resolve()))
        self.assertTrue(self.output_file.exists())

        df = pd.read_excel(self.output_file, sheet_name="Prehľad hotelov")

        expected_columns = [
            "Hotel",
            "Termín",
            "Noci",
            "Lead Time",
            "Hľadané osoby",
            "Počet ponúk",
            "Min. Booking Cena (€)",
            "Rozpätie cien Booking",
            "Priama Cena (€)",
            "Rozdiel (€)",
            "Rozdiel (%)",
            "Výhodnejšia ponuka",
            "Dostupnosť",
            "Poznámka",
        ]
        self.assertEqual(list(df.columns), expected_columns)
        self.assertEqual(len(df), 3)

        self.assertEqual(df.loc[0, "Hotel"], "Grand Hotel Kempinski")
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[0, "Min. Booking Cena (€)"], 250.0)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[0, "Priama Cena (€)"], 220.0)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[0, "Rozdiel (€)"], -30.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(df.loc[0, "Rozdiel (%)"], -0.12, places=4)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[0, "Dostupnosť"], "Dostupné")

        self.assertEqual(df.loc[1, "Hotel"], "Boutique Hotel Marrol's")
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[1, "Min. Booking Cena (€)"], 150.0)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[1, "Priama Cena (€)"], 190.0)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[1, "Rozdiel (€)"], 40.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(df.loc[1, "Rozdiel (%)"], 0.2667, places=4)
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[1, "Dostupnosť"], "Dostupné")

        self.assertEqual(df.loc[2, "Hotel"], "Carlton Hotel")
        self.assertTrue(pd.isna(df.loc[2, "Min. Booking Cena (€)"]))
        self.assertTrue(pd.isna(df.loc[2, "Priama Cena (€)"]))
        self.assertTrue(pd.isna(df.loc[2, "Rozdiel (€)"]))
        self.assertTrue(pd.isna(df.loc[2, "Rozdiel (%)"]))
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[2, "Výhodnejšia ponuka"], "Nedostupné")
        self.assertEqual(df.loc[2, "Dostupnosť"], "Vypredané")
        self.assertIn("Vypredané na oboch platformách", df.loc[2, "Poznámka"])

    def test_openpyxl_styles_and_conditional_fills(self) -> None:
        """Verify OpenPyXL cell fills: dark blue header and conditional delta fills."""
        self.generator.generate_comparison_report(
            results=self.sample_results,
            output_path=self.output_file,
        )

        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(self.output_file)
        ws = wb["Prehľad hotelov"]

        # Header Cell (A1)
        header_cell = ws.cell(row=1, column=1)
        self.assertIn("1B365D", str(header_cell.fill.start_color.rgb))
        self.assertTrue(header_cell.font.bold)
        self.assertEqual(header_cell.font.name, "Segoe UI")

        # Row 2 delta cell (Column 10 -> J2)
        delta_row2 = ws.cell(row=2, column=10)
        self.assertEqual(delta_row2.value, -30.0)
        self.assertIn("F8D7DA", str(delta_row2.fill.start_color.rgb))
        self.assertEqual(delta_row2.font.name, "Segoe UI")

        # Row 3 delta cell (Column 10 -> J3)
        delta_row3 = ws.cell(row=3, column=10)
        self.assertEqual(delta_row3.value, 40.0)
        self.assertIn("D4EDDA", str(delta_row3.fill.start_color.rgb))

        wb.close()

    def test_export_with_detailed_offers_sheet_and_room_matching(self) -> None:
        """Verify 'Detailné ponuky' includes room-matching columns, matched prices, and exclusive direct rows."""
        results_with_offers = [
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name="Grand Hotel Kempinski",
                booking_price=160.0,
                direct_price=150.0,
                offers=[
                    RoomOffer(
                        room_name="Štandardná izba",
                        occupancy=2,
                        price=160.0,
                        meal_plan="Raňajky v cene",
                        cancellation_policy="Bezplatné storno",
                        capacity_group="2_pax",
                        matched_room_name="Izba Classic",
                        matched_direct_price=155.0,
                        match_score=90,
                    ),
                    RoomOffer(
                        room_name="Apartmán Deluxe",
                        occupancy=2,
                        price=240.0,
                        meal_plan="Polpenzia",
                        cancellation_policy="Nevratná",
                        capacity_group="suite",
                        matched_room_name="Deluxe Suite",
                        matched_direct_price=260.0,
                        match_score=85,
                    ),
                ],
                direct_offers=[
                    RoomOffer(
                        room_name="Izba Classic",
                        price=155.0,
                        capacity_group="2_pax",
                        matched_room_name="Štandardná izba",
                    ),
                    RoomOffer(
                        room_name="Deluxe Suite",
                        price=260.0,
                        capacity_group="suite",
                        matched_room_name="Apartmán Deluxe",
                    ),
                    RoomOffer(
                        room_name="Rodinný apartmán 2+2",
                        price=320.0,
                        capacity_group="family",
                        matched_room_name=None,
                    ),
                ],
            )
        ]

        self.generator.generate_comparison_report(
            results=results_with_offers,
            output_path=self.output_file,
            include_details=True,
        )

        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(self.output_file)
        self.assertIn("Prehľad hotelov", wb.sheetnames)
        self.assertIn("Detailné ponuky", wb.sheetnames)

        ws_offers = wb["Detailné ponuky"]
        headers = [ws_offers.cell(row=1, column=c).value for c in range(1, 20)]
        self.assertEqual(headers[7], "Typ izby")
        self.assertEqual(headers[8], "Kapacitná skupina")
        self.assertEqual(headers[9], "Spárovaná priama izba")
        self.assertEqual(headers[13], "Booking Cena (€)")
        self.assertEqual(headers[14], "Priama Cena (€)")
        self.assertEqual(headers[15], "Rozdiel (€)")
        self.assertEqual(headers[17], "Výhodnejšie")

        # Row 2: uses matched_direct_price 155.0 instead of global direct_price 150.0 -> diff = +5.0 (Priamy web)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=8).value, "Štandardná izba")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=9).value, "2_pax")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=10).value, "Izba Classic")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=14).value, 160.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=15).value, 155.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=16).value, 5.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=18).value, "Priamy web")
        self.assertIn("D4EDDA", str(ws_offers.cell(row=2, column=16).fill.start_color.rgb))

        # Row 3: Apartmán Deluxe 240.0 vs 260.0 -> diff = -20.0 (Booking)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=8).value, "Apartmán Deluxe")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=9).value, "suite")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=10).value, "Deluxe Suite")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=15).value, 260.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=16).value, -20.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=18).value, "Booking")
        self.assertIn("F8D7DA", str(ws_offers.cell(row=3, column=16).fill.start_color.rgb))

        # Row 4: Exclusive direct family room below Booking offers
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=8).value, "Rodinný apartmán 2+2")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=9).value, "family")
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=10).value, "Exkluzívne priamy web")
        self.assertIsNone(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=14).value)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=15).value, 320.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws_offers.cell(row=4, column=18).value, "Exkluzívne priamy web")

        wb.close()

    def test_export_error_on_invalid_output_path(self) -> None:
        """Verify that file write error raises custom ExportError."""
        invalid_path = "Z:\\non_existent_drive_xyz\\report.xlsx"
        with self.assertRaises(ExportError):
            self.generator.generate_comparison_report(
                results=self.sample_results,
                output_path=invalid_path,
            )


if __name__ == "__main__":

"""Unit tests for Excel report generation and delta highlighting."""

from datetime import date
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest

import openpyxl

from core.models import ResultStatus, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from exporters.excel_generator import ExcelExporter, generate_excel_report


class TestExcelExporter(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for Excel report generation and styling."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.exports_dir = Path(self.test_dir) / "exports"
        self.exporter = ExcelExporter(exports_dir=self.exports_dir)

        self.sample_results = [
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h1",
                hotel_name="Grand Hotel",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 5),
                booking_price=300.0,
                direct_price=270.0,
                booking_url="https://booking.com/hotel/grand",
                direct_url="https://grandhotel.com/book",
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h2",
                hotel_name="Boutique Palace",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 5),
                booking_price=150.0,
                direct_price=180.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h3",
                hotel_name="City Hotel",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 5),
                booking_price=100.0,
                direct_price=100.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h4",
                hotel_name="Sold Out Inn",
                check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
                check_out=date(2026, 12, 5),
                booking_price=None,
                direct_price=None,
                is_available=False,
                error_message="Sold out on both channels",
                status=ResultStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE,
            ),
        ]

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        if Path(self.test_dir).exists():
            shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_generate_excel_report_structure_and_values(self) -> None:
        """Verify created Excel file contains expected rows, values, and formatting."""
        output_file = self.exports_dir / "test_report.xlsx"
        generated_path = self.exporter.generate_report(
            results=self.sample_results,
            output_path=output_file,
        )

        self.assertTrue(generated_path.exists())
        self.assertEqual(generated_path, output_file)

        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_file)
        self.assertIn("Prehľad hotelov", wb.sheetnames)
        self.assertIn("Detailné ponuky", wb.sheetnames)
        ws = wb["Prehľad hotelov"]

        # Check overview headers
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=1).value, "Hotel")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=2).value, "Termín")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=7).value, "Min. Booking Cena (€)")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=9).value, "Priama Cena (€)")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=10).value, "Rozdiel (€)")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=11).value, "Rozdiel (%)")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=12).value, "Výhodnejšia ponuka")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=13).value, "Dostupnosť")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=1, column=14).value, "Poznámka")

        # Row 2 (Grand Hotel): Booking 300, Direct 270 -> delta -30
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=2, column=1).value, "Grand Hotel")
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=2, column=7).value, 300.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=2, column=9).value, 270.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=2, column=10).value, -30.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(ws.cell(row=2, column=11).value, -0.10, places=4)

        delta_cell = ws.cell(row=2, column=10)
        self.assertIn("F8D7DA", str(delta_cell.fill.start_color.rgb))

        # Row 3 (Boutique Palace): Booking 150, Direct 180 -> delta +30
        delta_cell_3 = ws.cell(row=3, column=10)
        self.assertEqual(delta_cell_3.value, 30.0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(ws.cell(row=3, column=11).value, 0.20, places=4)
        self.assertIn("D4EDDA", str(delta_cell_3.fill.start_color.rgb))

        # Row 4 (City Hotel): Equal (0.0 EUR, 0.0 %)
        delta_cell_4 = ws.cell(row=4, column=10)
        self.assertEqual(delta_cell_4.value, 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(ws.cell(row=4, column=11).value, 0.0)
        self.assertIn("D1ECF1", str(delta_cell_4.fill.start_color.rgb))

        wb.close()

    def test_generate_empty_report(self) -> None:
        """Verify generating report with 0 results executes without error."""
        output_file = self.exports_dir / "empty_report.xlsx"
        generated_path = generate_excel_report([], output_path=output_file)

        self.assertTrue(Path(generated_path).exists())
        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(output_file)
        ws = wb["Prehľad hotelov"]
        self.assertEqual(ws.max_row, 1)
        wb.close()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

==================== FILE: ./tests/test_hotel_parsing.py ====================

"""Unit tests for hotel price parsing in scraper.booking_parser."""

import unittest
from typing import Optional

from scraper.booking_parser import clean_price_text


class TestHotelPriceParsing(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for clean_price_text helper function."""

    def test_european_price_formats(self) -> None:
        """Test parsing of common European price formats."""
        test_cases = [
            ("382,00 €", 382.0),
            ("382.00 EUR", 382.0),
            (" 1 250,50 € ", 1250.5),
        ]
        for raw_text, expected_price in test_cases:
            with self.subTest(raw=raw_text):
                result = clean_price_text(raw_text)
                self.assertEqual(result, expected_price)

    def test_price_with_surrounding_text(self) -> None:
        """Test parsing of prices embedded within surrounding sentences."""
        raw_text = "Celková cena: 450,00 € vrátane DPH"
        result = clean_price_text(raw_text)
        self.assertEqual(result, 450.0)

    def test_invalid_and_empty_inputs(self) -> None:
        """Test non-numeric, empty, or None inputs return None."""
        invalid_cases = [
            "",
            None,
            "Vypredané",
        ]
        for raw_text in invalid_cases:
            with self.subTest(raw=raw_text):
                result = clean_price_text(raw_text)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                self.assertIsNone(result)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_logger.py ====================

"""Unit tests for the centralized logger and Supabase sink."""

import os
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from loguru import logger
import requests

from core.logger import SupabaseSink, get_machine_id, setup_logger, shutdown_all_supabase_sinks


class TestLogger(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for Loguru setup and Supabase sink integration."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        logger.remove()
        shutdown_all_supabase_sinks(wait=True)
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.test_log_file = os.path.join(self.test_dir, "test_app.log")

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        logger.remove()
        shutdown_all_supabase_sinks(wait=True)
        if os.path.exists(self.test_dir):
            shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_get_machine_id(self) -> None:
        """Verify machine_id generation is deterministic and non-empty."""
        m_id1 = get_machine_id()
        m_id2 = get_machine_id()
        self.assertEqual(m_id1, m_id2)
        self.assertEqual(len(m_id1), 32)
        self.assertTrue(all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in m_id1))

    def test_local_file_logging(self) -> None:
        """Verify log messages are written to the rotating local log file."""
        setup_logger(
            log_file=self.test_log_file,
            enable_console=False,
            enable_supabase=False,
        )

        test_msg_info = "Informational test message #1234"
        test_msg_error = "Failure test message #5678"

        logger.info(test_msg_info)
        logger.error(test_msg_error)

        # Allow enqueue sink to flush
        logger.complete()
        time.sleep(0.1)

        self.assertTrue(os.path.exists(self.test_log_file))
        with open(self.test_log_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            content = f.read()

        self.assertIn(test_msg_info, content)
        self.assertIn(test_msg_error, content)
        self.assertIn("INFO", content)
        self.assertIn("ERROR", content)

    @patch("requests.post")
    def test_supabase_sink_dispatch_on_warning_and_error(self, mock_post: MagicMock) -> None:
        """Verify Supabase sink receives WARNING and ERROR but ignores INFO."""
        sink = SupabaseSink(
            supabase_url="https://xyzcompany.supabase.co",
            supabase_key="fake-anon-key-12345",
        )
        self.assertTrue(sink.is_configured)

        logger.remove()
        logger.add(sink, level="WARNING")
        mock_post.reset_mock()

        # 1. INFO message should be ignored by level="WARNING" filter
        logger.info("This is an info message that should NOT be sent to Supabase")
        time.sleep(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(mock_post.call_count, 0)

        # 2. WARNING message should trigger dispatch
        logger.warning("Warning: rate limit threshold approaching")
        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(mock_post.call_count, 1)

        # Inspect payload
        call_args = mock_post.call_args_list[0]
        url = call_args[0][0]
        kwargs = call_args[1]

        self.assertEqual(url, "https://xyzcompany.supabase.co/rest/v1/hs_app_logs")
        self.assertEqual(kwargs["json"]["level"], "WARNING")
        self.assertIn("rate limit threshold approaching", kwargs["json"]["message"])
        self.assertEqual(kwargs["json"]["machine_id"], sink.machine_id)
        self.assertEqual(kwargs["headers"]["apikey"], "fake-anon-key-12345")

        sink.shutdown(wait=True)

    @patch("requests.post")
    def test_supabase_sink_exception_traceback_capture(self, mock_post: MagicMock) -> None:
        """Verify unhandled exception stack traces are captured and formatted."""
        sink = SupabaseSink(
            supabase_url="https://xyzcompany.supabase.co",
            supabase_key="fake-anon-key-12345",
        )

        logger.remove()
        logger.add(sink, level="ERROR")
        mock_post.reset_mock()

        try:
            _ = 1 / 0
        except ZeroDivisionError:
            logger.opt(exception=True).error("Encountered division by zero")

        time.sleep(0.15)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(mock_post.call_count, 1)

        last_call = mock_post.call_args_list[-1]
        payload = last_call[1]["json"]
        self.assertEqual(payload["level"], "ERROR")
        self.assertIsNotNone(payload["traceback"])
        self.assertIn("ZeroDivisionError: division by zero", payload["traceback"])

        sink.shutdown(wait=True)

    @patch("requests.post", side_effect=requests.exceptions.ConnectionError("Network unreachable"))
    def test_supabase_sink_silent_failure_when_offline(self, mock_post: MagicMock) -> None:
        """Verify that offline / network connection failure never raises or crashes the app."""
        sink = SupabaseSink(
            supabase_url="https://xyzcompany.supabase.co",
            supabase_key="fake-anon-key-12345",
        )

        logger.remove()
        logger.add(sink, level="WARNING")

        # This should execute cleanly without raising any exception
        try:
            logger.error("Network is down test error")
            logger.critical("Critical outage simulation")
            time.sleep(0.15)
        except Exception as e:
            self.fail(f"Logger raised an exception during network failure: {e}")

        sink.shutdown(wait=True)

    @patch.dict(os.environ, {"SUPABASE_URL": "", "SUPABASE_KEY": "", "SUPABASE_ANON_KEY": ""}, clear=False)
    def test_unconfigured_supabase_sink_does_not_call_network(self) -> None:
        """Verify that missing credentials result in clean no-op."""
        sink = SupabaseSink(supabase_url="", supabase_key="")
        self.assertFalse(sink.is_configured)

        with patch("requests.post") as mock_post:
            mock_post.reset_mock()
            logger.remove()
            logger.add(sink, level="WARNING")
            logger.error("Should not send anywhere")
            time.sleep(0.1)
            mock_post.assert_not_called()

        sink.shutdown(wait=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_main.py ====================

"""Unit tests for main.py CLI arguments parsing and execution loop."""

from datetime import date, timedelta
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from main import main, parse_arguments


class TestMainCLI(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for CLI argument parser and entry point handling."""

    def test_default_cli_arguments(self) -> None:
        """Verify default CLI options produce valid stay dates."""
        with patch("sys.argv", ["main.py"]):
            args = parse_arguments()

        self.assertEqual(args.adults, 2)
        self.assertEqual(args.children, 0)
        self.assertIsNone(args.email)
        self.assertTrue(args.headless)
        self.assertEqual(args.output_dir, "reports")

    def test_custom_cli_arguments(self) -> None:
        """Verify custom CLI flags are parsed properly."""
        test_args = [
            "main.py",
            "--check-in", "2026-12-01",
            "--check-out", "2026-12-08",
            "--adults", "3",
            "--children", "1",
            "--email", "admin@hotels.com",
            "--hotels", "h1,h2",
            "--no-headless",
            "--min-jitter", "2.0",
            "--max-jitter", "4.0",
        ]
        with patch("sys.argv", test_args):
            args = parse_arguments()

        self.assertEqual(args.check_in, "2026-12-01")
        self.assertEqual(args.check_out, "2026-12-08")
        self.assertEqual(args.adults, 3)
        self.assertEqual(args.children, 1)
        self.assertEqual(args.email, "admin@hotels.com")
        self.assertEqual(args.hotels, "h1,h2")
        self.assertFalse(args.headless)
        self.assertEqual(args.min_jitter, 2.0)
        self.assertEqual(args.max_jitter, 4.0)

    def test_main_with_invalid_date_returns_code_1(self) -> None:
        """Invalid date format must be rejected with return code 1."""
        test_args = ["main.py", "--check-in", "invalid-date"]
        with patch("sys.argv", test_args):
            code = main()
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)

    def test_main_with_past_date_returns_code_1(self) -> None:
        """Check-in in the past must be rejected by ScrapingJob validation."""
        past_date = (date.today() - timedelta(days=5)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        test_args = ["main.py", "--check-in", past_date]
        with patch("sys.argv", test_args):
            code = main()
        self.assertEqual(code, 1)

    @patch("main.ScraperWorkerThread")
    def test_main_successful_execution_returns_code_0(self, mock_worker_cls: MagicMock) -> None:
        """Verify that successful execution through queue loop exits with code 0."""
        mock_worker = MagicMock()
        mock_worker.is_alive.return_value = False
        mock_worker_cls.return_value = mock_worker

        tomorrow = (date.today() + timedelta(days=1)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        next_week = (date.today() + timedelta(days=5)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")

        test_args = ["main.py", "--check-in", tomorrow, "--check-out", next_week]
        with patch("sys.argv", test_args):
            code = main()

        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        mock_worker.start.assert_called_once()
        mock_worker.join.assert_called_once()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_models.py ====================

"""Unit tests for core models (ScrapingJob and ScrapedResult)."""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
from datetime import date, timedelta
import unittest

from core.models import EMAIL_REGEX, ResultStatus, RoomOffer, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob


class TestScrapingJob(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for ScrapingJob model and validations."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.today = date.today()
        self.tomorrow = self.today + timedelta(days=1)
        self.next_week = self.today + timedelta(days=7)

    def test_valid_job_creation(self) -> None:
        """Verify successful initialization with valid attributes."""
        job = ScrapingJob(
            check_in=self.tomorrow,
            check_out=self.next_week,
            adults=2,
            children=1,
            email="test.user@example.com",
        )
        self.assertEqual(job.check_in, self.tomorrow)
        self.assertEqual(job.check_out, self.next_week)
        self.assertEqual(job.adults, 2)
        self.assertEqual(job.children, 1)
        self.assertEqual(job.email, "test.user@example.com")
        self.assertEqual(job.nights, 6)
        self.assertEqual(job.total_guests, 3)
        self.assertTrue(job.job_id)

    def test_immutability(self) -> None:
        """Verify that ScrapingJob is strictly immutable."""
        job = ScrapingJob(
            check_in=self.tomorrow,
            check_out=self.next_week,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            job.adults = 4  # type: ignore[misc]

    def test_past_check_in_rejected(self) -> None:
        """Check-in date in the past must raise ValueError."""
        yesterday = self.today - timedelta(days=1)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError) as ctx:
            ScrapingJob(
                check_in=yesterday,
                check_out=self.tomorrow,
            )
        self.assertIn("cannot be in the past", str(ctx.exception))

    def test_check_out_not_after_check_in_rejected(self) -> None:
        """Check-out equal to or before check-in must raise ValueError."""
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(
                check_in=self.tomorrow,
                check_out=self.tomorrow,
            )

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(
                check_in=self.next_week,
                check_out=self.tomorrow,
            )

    def test_invalid_date_types_rejected(self) -> None:
        """Non-date types must raise TypeError."""
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapingJob(
                check_in="2026-12-01",  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                check_out=self.next_week,
            )

    def test_adults_validation(self) -> None:
        """Adult count must be integer >= 1 and <= 50."""
        # Zero adults
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, adults=0)

        # Negative adults
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, adults=-1)

        # Exceeding limit
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, adults=51)

        # Non-int type (boolean or float)
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, adults=True)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, adults=2.5)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_children_validation(self) -> None:
        """Children count must be integer >= 0 and <= 50."""
        # Negative children
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, children=-1)

        # Exceeding limit
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, children=51)

        # Non-int type
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapingJob(check_in=self.tomorrow, check_out=self.next_week, children=False)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_email_validation(self) -> None:
        """Validate email handling: valid emails pass, invalid raise ValueError/TypeError."""
        # Valid variations
        valid_emails = [
            "user@domain.com",
            "first.last+tag@sub.domain.co.uk",
            "admin_123@hotel-booking.org",
        ]
        for email in valid_emails:
            job = ScrapingJob(
                check_in=self.tomorrow,
                check_out=self.next_week,
                email=email,
            )
            self.assertEqual(job.email, email)

        # Invalid emails
        invalid_emails = [
            "not-an-email",
            "@missing-local.com",
            "missing-domain@",
            "spaces in@domain.com",
            "",
            "   ",
        ]
        for email in invalid_emails:
            with self.subTest(email=email):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    ScrapingJob(
                        check_in=self.tomorrow,
                        check_out=self.next_week,
                        email=email,
                    )

        # Non-string email
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapingJob(
                check_in=self.tomorrow,
                check_out=self.next_week,
                email=12345,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )


class TestScrapedResult(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for ScrapedResult model, validations, and calculations."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.today = date.today()
        self.check_in = self.today + timedelta(days=2)
        self.check_out = self.today + timedelta(days=4)

    def test_valid_scraped_result_creation(self) -> None:
        """Verify proper initialization and default attributes."""
        res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="grand_hotel_01",
            hotel_name="Grand Hotel Kempinski",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            booking_price=420.0,
            direct_price=380.0,
            currency="EUR",
        )
        self.assertEqual(res.hotel_id, "grand_hotel_01")
        self.assertEqual(res.status, ResultStatus.SUCCESS)
        self.assertEqual(res.price_delta, -40.0)
        self.assertEqual(res.price_delta_percent, -9.52)
        self.assertEqual(res.cheaper_channel, "DIRECT")

    def test_immutability(self) -> None:
        """Verify ScrapedResult is immutable."""
        res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h1",
            hotel_name="Test Hotel",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
        )
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            res.booking_price = 100.0  # type: ignore[misc]

    def test_price_delta_calculations(self) -> None:
        """Verify price_delta and cheaper_channel edge cases."""
        # Direct cheaper
        res1 = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h1",
            hotel_name="Hotel A",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            booking_price=200.0,
            direct_price=180.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(res1.price_delta, -20.0)
        self.assertEqual(res1.cheaper_channel, "DIRECT")

        # Booking cheaper
        res2 = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h2",
            hotel_name="Hotel B",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            booking_price=150.0,
            direct_price=175.50,
        )
        self.assertEqual(res2.price_delta, 25.50)
        self.assertEqual(res2.cheaper_channel, "BOOKING")

        # Equal prices
        res3 = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h3",
            hotel_name="Hotel C",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            booking_price=100.0,
            direct_price=100.0,
        )
        self.assertEqual(res3.price_delta, 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(res3.cheaper_channel, "EQUAL")

        # Missing price in one channel
        res4 = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h4",
            hotel_name="Hotel D",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            booking_price=100.0,
            direct_price=None,
        )
        self.assertIsNone(res4.price_delta)
        self.assertIsNone(res4.price_delta_percent)
        self.assertIsNone(res4.cheaper_channel)

    def test_negative_prices_rejected(self) -> None:
        """Negative prices must raise ValueError."""
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h1",
                hotel_name="Hotel A",
                check_in=self.check_in,
                check_out=self.check_out,
                booking_price=-10.0,
            )

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h1",
                hotel_name="Hotel A",
                check_in=self.check_in,
                check_out=self.check_out,
                direct_price=-5.0,
            )

    def test_status_normalization_and_validation(self) -> None:
        """Ensure string status is coerced to ResultStatus or rejected if invalid."""
        res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h1",
            hotel_name="Hotel A",
            check_in=self.check_in,
            check_out=self.check_out,
            status="PARTIAL",  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )
        self.assertEqual(res.status, ResultStatus.PARTIAL)

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h1",
                hotel_name="Hotel A",
                check_in=self.check_in,
                check_out=self.check_out,
                status="UNKNOWN_STATUS",  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )

    def test_offers_field_and_lowest_price_calculation(self) -> None:
        """Verify that offers list is stored and auto-populates booking_price if omitted."""
        offer1 = RoomOffer(room_name="Izba Deluxe", price=250.0, meal_plan="Raňajky v cene")
        offer2 = RoomOffer(room_name="Štandardná izba", price=180.0, cancellation_policy="Bezplatné storno")

        res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h1",
            hotel_name="Grand Hotel",
            offers=[offer1, offer2],
        )

        self.assertEqual(len(res.offers), 2)
        self.assertEqual(res.booking_price, 180.0)
        self.assertEqual(res.offers[0].room_name, "Izba Deluxe")
        self.assertEqual(res.offers[1].cancellation_policy, "Bezplatné storno")

    def test_invalid_offers_type_rejected(self) -> None:
        """Non-list or lists containing non-RoomOffer objects must raise TypeError."""
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name="Hotel X",
                offers="not-a-list",  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            )

        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_name="Hotel X",
                offers=[{"room": "deluxe", "price": 100}],  # type: ignore[list-item]
            )


class TestRoomOffer(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for RoomOffer dataclass."""

    def test_valid_room_offer_creation(self) -> None:
        """Valid room offer should instantiate properly."""
        offer = RoomOffer(
            room_name="Apartmán s výhľadom na jazero",
            occupancy=3,
            price=299.99,
            currency="EUR",
            meal_plan="Polpenzia",
            cancellation_policy="Storno do 24h pred príchodom",
        )
        self.assertEqual(offer.room_name, "Apartmán s výhľadom na jazero")
        self.assertEqual(offer.occupancy, 3)
        self.assertEqual(offer.price, 299.99)
        self.assertEqual(offer.currency, "EUR")
        self.assertEqual(offer.meal_plan, "Polpenzia")
        self.assertEqual(offer.cancellation_policy, "Storno do 24h pred príchodom")

    def test_room_offer_validation(self) -> None:
        """Invalid room_name, occupancy, or negative price must raise errors."""
        # Empty name
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="", price=100.0)

        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="   ", price=100.0)

        # Invalid occupancy
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", occupancy=0, price=100.0)

        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", occupancy="two", price=100.0)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

        # Negative price
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", price=-50.0)

        # Non-numeric price
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", price="100")  # type: ignore[arg-type]

        # Invalid meal_plan type
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", price=100.0, meal_plan=123)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_room_offer_immutability(self) -> None:
        """RoomOffer must be strictly immutable."""
        offer = RoomOffer(room_name="Izba", price=100.0)
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            offer.price = 120.0  # type: ignore[misc]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_parsers.py ====================

"""Unit and integration tests for Booking.com dynamic parser."""

from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest

from core.models import ResultStatus
from scraper.booking_parser import BookingParser, clean_price_text
from scraper.browser import BrowserManager


class TestPriceCleaner(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for price cleaning and normalization helper."""

    def test_clean_price_formats(self) -> None:
        """Verify handling of diverse European and international price formats."""
        cases = [
            ("120 €", None, 120.0),
            ("€ 149.99", None, 149.99),
            ("1 250,50 €", None, 1250.50),
            ("1.450,00 EUR", None, 1450.0),
            ("1,450.00 EUR", None, 1450.0),
            ("EUR 89", None, 89.0),
            ("Price: 320,5 €", r"(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)", 320.5),
            ("Total: 500 € for 2 nights", r"(\d+)\s*€", 500.0),
        ]
        for raw, regex, expected in cases:
            with self.subTest(raw=raw):
                self.assertEqual(clean_price_text(raw, price_regex=regex), expected)

    def test_invalid_price_returns_none(self) -> None:
        """Non-numeric strings or empty strings must return None."""
        self.assertIsNone(clean_price_text(""))
        self.assertIsNone(clean_price_text("Sold out"))
        self.assertIsNone(clean_price_text(None))  # type: ignore[arg-type]


class TestBookingParser(unittest.TestCase):
    """Integration test suite for BookingParser using Playwright headless context."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
        cls.browser_manager = BrowserManager(headless=True)
        cls.browser_manager.launch_browser()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:
        cls.browser_manager.close()

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.screenshots_dir = Path(self.test_dir) / "screenshots"
        self.parser = BookingParser(screenshots_dir=self.screenshots_dir)
        self.page = self.browser_manager.new_page()

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.page.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        if Path(self.test_dir).exists():
            shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_extract_price_success(self) -> None:
        """Verify successful price extraction when valid price element is present."""
        html = """
        <html>
            <body>
                <div class="hotel-card">
                    <h2>Grand Hotel River Park</h2>
                    <div class="bui-price-display__value">185,50&nbsp;€</div>
                </div>
            </body>
        </html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "sold_out_selectors": [".sold-out-banner", ".bui-alert--error"],
            "price_selectors": [
                ".non-existent-selector",
                ".bui-price-display__value",
            ],
            "price_regex": r"([\d\s.,]+)",
        }

        result = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Grand Hotel River Park",
        )

        self.assertEqual(result.hotel_name, "Grand Hotel River Park")
        self.assertTrue(result.is_available)
        self.assertEqual(result.booking_price, 185.50)
        self.assertIsNone(result.error_message)
        self.assertEqual(result.status, ResultStatus.SUCCESS)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.offers), 1)

    def test_extract_multiple_room_offers_success(self) -> None:
        """Verify extraction of multiple room offers with detailed meal plans and cancellation policies."""
        html = """
        <html>
            <body>
                <h2>Grand Hotel Kempinski High Tatras</h2>
                <table class="hprt-table">
                    <tbody>
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td>
                                <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Štandardná dvojlôžková izba</a>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <div class="hprt-condition-detail">Raňajky v cene</div>
                                <div class="hprt-cancellation-policy">Bezplatné storno</div>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">160,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td>
                                <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Apartmán Deluxe</a>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <div class="hprt-condition-detail">Polpenzia</div>
                                <div class="hprt-cancellation-policy">Nevratná platba</div>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">280,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </body>
        </html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "room_row_selectors": ["table.hprt-table tbody tr.js-rt-block-row"],
            "room_name_selectors": [".hprt-roomtype-link"],
            "price_selectors": [".bui-price-display__value"],
            "meal_plan_selectors": [".hprt-condition-detail"],
            "cancellation_selectors": [".hprt-cancellation-policy"],
            "price_regex": r"([\d\s.,]+)",
        }

        result = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Grand Hotel Kempinski High Tatras",
        )

        self.assertTrue(result.is_available)
        self.assertEqual(result.booking_price, 160.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.offers), 2)

        offer1 = result.offers[0]
        self.assertEqual(offer1.room_name, "Štandardná dvojlôžková izba")
        self.assertEqual(offer1.price, 160.0)
        self.assertEqual(offer1.meal_plan, "Raňajky v cene")
        self.assertEqual(offer1.cancellation_policy, "Bezplatné storno")

        offer2 = result.offers[1]
        self.assertEqual(offer2.room_name, "Apartmán Deluxe")
        self.assertEqual(offer2.price, 280.0)
        self.assertEqual(offer2.meal_plan, "Polpenzia")
        self.assertEqual(offer2.cancellation_policy, "Nevratná platba")

    def test_extract_room_offers_filters_by_requested_guests(self) -> None:
        """Verify strict capacity filtering: offers for 1 or 4 guests are excluded when 2 guests requested."""
        html = """
        <html>
            <body>
                <h2>Hotel Grand Capacity Test</h2>
                <table class="hprt-table">
                    <tbody>
                        <!-- 1 Guest Room -->
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td>
                                <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Jednolôžková izba Economy</a>
                            </td>
                            <td class="hprt-table-cell-occupancy">
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy" title="1 hosť"></span>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">90,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        <!-- 2 Guests Room: Base rate -->
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td>
                                <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Dvojlôžková izba Standard</a>
                            </td>
                            <td class="hprt-table-cell-occupancy">
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">140,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        <!-- 2 Guests Room: Rate with breakfast (inherits room name and occupancy) -->
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td></td>
                            <td></td>
                            <td>
                                <div class="hprt-condition-detail">Raňajky v cene</div>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">165,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                        <!-- 4 Guests Room: Family suite -->
                        <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                            <td>
                                <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Rodinný Apartmán</a>
                            </td>
                            <td class="hprt-table-cell-occupancy" aria-label="Max. počet hostí: 4">
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                                <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                            </td>
                            <td>
                                <span class="bui-price-display__value">310,00&nbsp;€</span>
                            </td>
                        </tr>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </body>
        </html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "room_row_selectors": ["table.hprt-table tbody tr.js-rt-block-row"],
            "occupancy_selectors": ["td.hprt-table-cell-occupancy"],
            "occupancy_icon_selectors": [".bicon-occupancy"],
            "room_name_selectors": [".hprt-roomtype-link"],
            "price_selectors": [".bui-price-display__value"],
            "meal_plan_selectors": [".hprt-condition-detail"],
            "price_regex": r"([\d\s.,]+)",
        }

        # 1. Test requested_guests = 2
        result_2 = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Hotel Grand Capacity Test",
            requested_guests=2,
        )
        self.assertTrue(result_2.is_available)
        self.assertEqual(len(result_2.offers), 2)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.booking_price, 140.0)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[0].occupancy, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[0].price, 140.0)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[1].occupancy, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[1].price, 165.0)
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[1].room_name, "Dvojlôžková izba Standard")
        self.assertEqual(result_2.offers[1].meal_plan, "Raňajky v cene")

        # 2. Test requested_guests = 4
        result_4 = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Hotel Grand Capacity Test",
            requested_guests=4,
        )
        self.assertTrue(result_4.is_available)
        self.assertEqual(len(result_4.offers), 1)
        self.assertEqual(result_4.booking_price, 310.0)
        self.assertEqual(result_4.offers[0].room_name, "Rodinný Apartmán")
        self.assertEqual(result_4.offers[0].occupancy, 4)

        # 3. Test requested_guests = 5 (No offers match)
        result_5 = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Hotel Grand Capacity Test",
            requested_guests=5,
        )
        self.assertFalse(result_5.is_available)
        self.assertIsNone(result_5.booking_price)
        self.assertEqual(len(result_5.offers), 0)
        self.assertIn("Cena ani stav nedostupnosti neboli identifikované", result_5.error_message or "")
        self.assertIn("5", result_5.error_message or "")

    def test_detect_sold_out_banner(self) -> None:
        """Verify sold out detection returns is_available=False and booking_price=None."""
        html = """
        <html>
            <body>
                <div class="hotel-header">
                    <h2>Boutique Hotel Marrol's</h2>
                    <div class="sold-out-alert">Žiaľ, v tomto ubytovaní nie sú voľné izby</div>
                    <div class="hidden-old-price" style="display:none">120 €</div>
                </div>
            </body>
        </html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "sold_out_selectors": [".sold-out-alert", ".no-availability-banner"],
            "price_selectors": [".hidden-old-price"],
        }

        result = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Boutique Hotel Marrol's",
        )

        self.assertEqual(result.hotel_name, "Boutique Hotel Marrol's")
        self.assertFalse(result.is_available)
        self.assertIsNone(result.booking_price)
        self.assertIsNone(result.error_message)
        self.assertEqual(result.status, ResultStatus.NOT_AVAILABLE)

    def test_fallback_when_price_and_sold_out_missing(self) -> None:
        """When neither price nor sold-out is identified, save screenshot and return warning error."""
        html = """
        <html>
            <body>
                <div class="unknown-layout">
                    <h2>Unknown Layout Hotel</h2>
                    <p>Some unrelated text without pricing or availability</p>
                </div>
            </body>
        </html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "sold_out_selectors": [".sold-out-alert"],
            "price_selectors": [".price-tag"],
        }

        result = self.parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page,
            rules=rules,
            hotel_name="Unknown Layout Hotel",
        )

        self.assertEqual(result.hotel_name, "Unknown Layout Hotel")
        self.assertFalse(result.is_available)
        self.assertIsNone(result.booking_price)
        self.assertIn("Cena ani stav nedostupnosti neboli identifikované", result.error_message or "")
        self.assertEqual(result.status, ResultStatus.FAILED)

        # Verify diagnostic screenshot was created
        saved_screenshots = list(self.screenshots_dir.glob("booking_unknown_layout_hotel_*.png"))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(saved_screenshots), 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_queue_manager.py ====================

"""Unit tests for thread-safe QueueManager."""

from datetime import date
import unittest

from core.models import ResultStatus, ScrapedResult
from services.queue_manager import EventType, ProgressEvent, QueueManager


class TestQueueManager(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for event queue dispatch and cancellation signals."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.qm = QueueManager(maxsize=50)

    def test_publish_and_retrieve_event(self) -> None:
        """Verify FIFO publishing and retrieval of ProgressEvent."""
        self.qm.publish_job_started(total_hotels=5)
        event = self.qm.get_event(block=False)
        self.assertIsNotNone(event)
        assert event is not None
        self.assertEqual(event.event_type, EventType.JOB_STARTED)
        self.assertEqual(event.percentage, 0.0)
        self.assertIn("Total hotels: 5", event.message)

    def test_hotel_progress_events(self) -> None:
        """Verify hotel start and completion event dispatches."""
        self.qm.publish_hotel_started("Hotel Grand", 1, 4)
        e1 = self.qm.get_event(block=False)
        self.assertIsNotNone(e1)
        assert e1 is not None
        self.assertEqual(e1.event_type, EventType.HOTEL_STARTED)
        self.assertEqual(e1.percentage, 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(e1.current_hotel, "Hotel Grand")

        res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_id="h1",
            hotel_name="Hotel Grand",
            check_in=date(2026, 12, 1),
            check_out=date(2026, 12, 5),
            booking_price=200.0,
            direct_price=180.0,
            status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
        )
        self.qm.publish_hotel_completed("Hotel Grand", 1, 4, res)
        e2 = self.qm.get_event(block=False)
        self.assertIsNotNone(e2)
        assert e2 is not None
        self.assertEqual(e2.event_type, EventType.HOTEL_COMPLETED)
        self.assertEqual(e2.percentage, 25.0)
        self.assertEqual(e2.result, res)

    def test_cancellation_flow(self) -> None:
        """Verify cancellation request signaling and reset."""
        self.assertFalse(self.qm.is_stop_requested())
        self.qm.request_stop()
        self.assertTrue(self.qm.is_stop_requested())

        self.qm.publish_job_stopped("Interrupted by operator")
        e = self.qm.get_event(block=False)
        self.assertIsNotNone(e)
        assert e is not None
        self.assertEqual(e.event_type, EventType.JOB_STOPPED)

        self.qm.reset()
        self.assertFalse(self.qm.is_stop_requested())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/test_room_matcher.py ====================

"""Unit and integration tests for room catalog sync, RoomMatcher, and deterministic mapping."""

from dataclasses import FrozenInstanceError
from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from core.models import ResultStatus, RoomOffer, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from core.room_matcher import RoomMatcher
from main import main, parse_arguments
from scraper.booking_parser import BookingParser
from scraper.browser import BrowserManager
from scraper.direct_parser import DirectParser
from scraper.engine import ScrapingEngine


class TestRoomMatcherLogic(unittest.TestCase):
    """Unit tests for RoomMatcher classification, catalog matching, and runtime application."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.matcher = RoomMatcher()
        self.hotel = {
            "id": "hotel_permon",
            "name": "Grand Hotel Permon",
            "matching_rules": {
                "min_fuzzy_score": 60,
                "synonyms": {"manzelska": "dvojlozkova"},
                "stop_words": ["izba", "hotel"],
            },
        }

    def test_capacity_group_classification_and_priority(self) -> None:
        """Verify priority order: family > suite > 2_plus_1 > 1_pax > 2_pax."""
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Rodinný apartmán 2+2"),
            "family",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Apartmán Deluxe"),
            "suite",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Dvojlôžková izba s prístelkou"),
            "2_plus_1",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Jednolôžková izba Štandard"),
            "1_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group(
                "Izba Štandard", bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ"
            ),
            "2_pax",
        )
        # Ensure 'booking' does not falsely trigger 'king'
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Jednolôžková izba Booking Promo"),
            "1_pax",
        )

    def test_build_catalog_mappings_respects_capacity_boundaries_and_family_exclusivity(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        """Rooms only match within identical capacity_group; family is exclusive direct."""
        booking_offers = [
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Jednolôžková izba Standard",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 jednolôžková posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Standard",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Apartmán Prezidentský",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 extra veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
        ]
        direct_offers = [
            RoomOffer(room_name="Jednolôžková izba Štandard", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Štandard s výhľadom", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Rodinná izba 2+2", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Prezidentský apartmán", price=0.0),
        ]

        mappings = self.matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
            hotel=self.hotel,
            booking_offers=booking_offers,
            direct_offers=direct_offers,
            global_rules={"min_fuzzy_score": 65},
            existing_mappings=[],
        )

        self.assertEqual(len(mappings), 4)
        by_pattern = {m["direct_room_pattern"]: m for m in mappings}

        # 1_pax must match 1_pax (not 2_pax)
        m_1pax = by_pattern["Jednolôžková izba Štandard"]
        self.assertEqual(m_1pax["capacity_group"], "1_pax")
        self.assertEqual(m_1pax["booking_room_name"], "Jednolôžková izba Standard")
        self.assertFalse(m_1pax["is_exclusive_direct"])
        self.assertGreaterEqual(m_1pax["match_score"], 60)

        # 2_pax matches 2_pax
        m_2pax = by_pattern["Dvojlôžková izba Štandard s výhľadom"]
        self.assertEqual(m_2pax["capacity_group"], "2_pax")
        self.assertEqual(m_2pax["booking_room_name"], "Dvojlôžková izba Standard")
        self.assertFalse(m_2pax["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # family must stay exclusive direct
        m_family = by_pattern["Rodinná izba 2+2"]
        self.assertEqual(m_family["capacity_group"], "family")
        self.assertIsNone(m_family["booking_room_name"])
        self.assertTrue(m_family["is_exclusive_direct"])
        self.assertEqual(m_family["match_score"], 0)

        # suite matches suite
        m_suite = by_pattern["Prezidentský apartmán"]
        self.assertEqual(m_suite["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertEqual(m_suite["booking_room_name"], "Apartmán Prezidentský")
        self.assertFalse(m_suite["is_exclusive_direct"])

    def test_permon_capacity_group_classification(self) -> None:
        """Verify Permon capacity rules: PLUS->2_plus_1, Suita->suite, bed_info->1_pax, family."""
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"),
            "2_plus_1",
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"),
            "2_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Premium PLUS"),
            "2_plus_1",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Deluxe Premium PLUS"),
            "2_plus_1",
        )

        # Suita Premium Deluxe must ALWAYS be suite, never 2_pax or 2_plus_1
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Suita Premium Deluxe"),
            "suite",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group(
                "Suita Premium Deluxe",
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
            ),
            "suite",
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Suita Premium Deluxe"),
            "2_pax",
        )

        # Family always wins over PLUS / Suite
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Deluxe Family PLUS"),
            "family",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Suite Deluxe Family"),
            "family",
        )

        # 1_pax via direct name or Booking bed_info (takes priority over sofa/2_plus_1)
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"),
            "1_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group(
                "Izba Economy", bed_info="1 jednolôžková posteľ"
            ),
            "1_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group(
                "Izba Economy", bed_info="1 jednolôžková posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka"
            ),
            "1_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group(
                "Izba s 2 oddelenými posteľami", bed_info="2 jednolôžkové postele"
            ),
            "2_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("2-lôžková Premium Mini"),
            "2_pax",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            self.matcher.classify_capacity_group("Malá izba s dvojlôžkom"),
            "2_pax",
        )

    def test_fuzzy_score_58_68_and_n_to_1_fallback_within_group(self) -> None:
        """Verify lowered min_fuzzy_score=55, attribute normalization, and N:1 fallback."""
        hotel = {
            "id": "hotel_permon",
            "name": "Grand Hotel Permon",
            "matching_rules": {},
        }
        # Legacy global min_fuzzy_score=70 falls back to default 55
        merged = self.matcher.merge_rules(global_rules={"min_fuzzy_score": 70}, hotel_rules={})
        self.assertEqual(merged["min_fuzzy_score"], 55)

        booking_offers = [
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Suita Deluxe Kriváň",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Prezidentská suita",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 extra veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Izba Premium s balkónom",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
            ),
        ]
        direct_offers = [
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe Kriváň", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Prestige", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Unikátna Kategória", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium PLUS", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Špeciálna Ponuka PLUS", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Deluxe Family PLUS", price=0.0),
        ]

        mappings = self.matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
            hotel=hotel,
            booking_offers=booking_offers,
            direct_offers=direct_offers,
            global_rules={"min_fuzzy_score": 70},
            existing_mappings=[],
        )
        by_pat = {m["direct_room_pattern"]: m for m in mappings}

        # Normalized attributes give high score for Suite Deluxe Kriváň vs Suita Deluxe Kriváň
        m_deluxe_krivan = by_pat["Suite Deluxe Kriváň"]
        self.assertEqual(m_deluxe_krivan["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertEqual(m_deluxe_krivan["booking_room_name"], "Suita Deluxe Kriváň")
        self.assertEqual(m_deluxe_krivan["booking_bed_info"], "1 manželská posteľ")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(m_deluxe_krivan["match_score"], 90)

        # Score in 58-68 range passes threshold (>= 55)
        m_prestige = by_pat["Suite Prestige"]
        self.assertEqual(m_prestige["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertEqual(m_prestige["booking_room_name"], "Prezidentská suita")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(m_prestige["match_score"], 58)
        self.assertLessEqual(m_prestige["match_score"], 68)
        self.assertFalse(m_prestige["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # 2-lôžková Premium PLUS -> 2_plus_1, high score against Izba Premium s balkónom
        m_prem_plus = by_pat["2-lôžková Premium PLUS"]
        self.assertEqual(m_prem_plus["capacity_group"], "2_plus_1")
        self.assertEqual(m_prem_plus["booking_room_name"], "Izba Premium s balkónom")
        self.assertEqual(
            m_prem_plus["booking_bed_info"],
