            "1 manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
        )
        self.assertGreaterEqual(m_prem_plus["match_score"], 80)

        # N:1 fallback in same group when best_score < 55
        m_suite_fallback = by_pat["Suite Unikátna Kategória"]
        self.assertEqual(m_suite_fallback["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertIsNotNone(m_suite_fallback["booking_room_name"])
        self.assertLess(m_suite_fallback["match_score"], 55)
        self.assertFalse(m_suite_fallback["is_exclusive_direct"])

        m_2p1_fallback = by_pat["2-lôžková Špeciálna Ponuka PLUS"]
        self.assertEqual(m_2p1_fallback["capacity_group"], "2_plus_1")
        self.assertEqual(m_2p1_fallback["booking_room_name"], "Izba Premium s balkónom")
        self.assertLess(m_2p1_fallback["match_score"], 55)
        self.assertFalse(m_2p1_fallback["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # family stays exclusive direct even if candidates exist
        m_family = by_pat["2-lôžková Deluxe Family PLUS"]
        self.assertEqual(m_family["capacity_group"], "family")
        self.assertIsNone(m_family["booking_room_name"])
        self.assertIsNone(m_family["booking_bed_info"])
        self.assertTrue(m_family["is_exclusive_direct"])

    def test_build_catalog_mappings_preserves_manual_override(self) -> None:
        """Manual override entries in existing_mappings must not be overwritten."""
        booking_offers = [
            RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Standard", price=0.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Superior", price=0.0),
        ]
        direct_offers = [
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba Classic", price=0.0),
        ]
        existing = [
            {
                "hotel_id": "hotel_permon",
                "capacity_group": "suite",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Classic",
                "booking_room_name": "Dvojlôžková izba Superior",
                "is_manual_override": True,
                "is_exclusive_direct": False,
                "match_score": 100,
                "priority": 1,
                "is_active": True,
            }
        ]

        mappings = self.matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
            hotel=self.hotel,
            booking_offers=booking_offers,
            direct_offers=direct_offers,
            global_rules={},
            existing_mappings=existing,
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(mappings), 1)
        self.assertEqual(mappings[0]["booking_room_name"], "Dvojlôžková izba Superior")
        self.assertEqual(mappings[0]["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertTrue(mappings[0]["is_manual_override"])

    def test_apply_saved_mappings_deterministic_and_immutable(self) -> None:
        """Daily scraping uses saved mappings deterministically without fuzzy matching."""
        booking_offers = [
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Standard",
                price=240.0,
                meal_plan="Raňajky",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Standard",
                price=275.0,
                meal_plan="Polpenzia",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Nezmapovaná izba",
                price=199.0,
            ),
        ]
        direct_offers = [
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba Štandard", price=210.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Izba Štandard Promo", price=195.0),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Rodinná izba 2+2", price=340.0),
        ]
        saved_mappings = [
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "2_pax",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Štandard",
                "booking_room_name": "Dvojlôžková izba Standard",
                "match_score": 88,
                "priority": 1,
                "is_active": True,
            },
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "2_pax",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Štandard Promo",
                "booking_room_name": "Dvojlôžková izba Standard",
                "match_score": 84,
                "priority": 2,
                "is_active": True,
            },
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "family",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Rodinná izba 2+2",
                "booking_room_name": None,
                "match_score": 0,
                "is_exclusive_direct": True,
                "priority": 3,
                "is_active": True,
            },
        ]

        out_booking, out_direct = self.matcher.apply_saved_mappings(
            booking_offers, direct_offers, saved_mappings
        )

        # Immutable frozen dataclass preserved
        with self.assertRaises(FrozenInstanceError):
            out_booking[0].matched_direct_price = 999.0  # type: ignore[misc]

        # N:1 picks cheapest matching direct offer (195.0)
        self.assertEqual(out_booking[0].capacity_group, "2_pax")
        self.assertEqual(out_booking[0].matched_room_name, "Izba Štandard Promo")
        self.assertEqual(out_booking[0].matched_direct_price, 195.0)
        self.assertEqual(out_booking[0].match_score, 84)

        self.assertEqual(out_booking[1].matched_direct_price, 195.0)
        self.assertIsNone(out_booking[2].matched_room_name)
        self.assertIsNone(out_booking[2].matched_direct_price)

        # Direct offers enriched with capacity_group and paired booking room name
        self.assertEqual(out_direct[0].capacity_group, "2_pax")
        self.assertEqual(out_direct[0].matched_room_name, "Dvojlôžková izba Standard")
        self.assertEqual(out_direct[2].capacity_group, "family")
        self.assertIsNone(out_direct[2].matched_room_name)

    def test_permon_full_catalog_capacity_first_matching(self) -> None:
        """Integration test with real Grand Hotel Permon Booking vs Direct catalogs."""
        hotel = {
            "id": "8235402c-05a2-4b18-b691-f25a18c7ad73",
            "name": "Grand Hotel Permon",
            "matching_rules": {},
        }
        booking_offers = [
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Malá izba s dvojlôžkom alebo s 2 oddelenými lôžkami",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 jednolôžková posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Izba Premium Mini s balkónom",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Izba Deluxe s balkónom",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 pohovka + 1 veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Izba Premium s výhľadom na hory",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 pohovka + 1 veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Izba Premium Kriváň s balkónom",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 pohovka + 1 veľká manželská posteľ",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Apartmán s výhľadom na hory",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Suita Prestige",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Prezidentská suita",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Suita Penthouse",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
            RoomOffer(
                room_name="Suita Premium Deluxe",
                price=0.0,
                bed_info="1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 pohovka",
            ),
        ]
        direct_offers = [
            # 1_pax (2)
            RoomOffer(room_name="1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="1-lôžková Premium Kriváň/Premium „Kriváň“ Grande"),
            # 2_pax (2)
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium Mini"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium Kriváň Mini"),
            # 2_plus_1 (6)
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium s prístelkou"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium PLUS"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium Kriváň s prístelkou"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Premium Kriváň PLUS"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Deluxe Premium PLUS"),
            # family (2)
            RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Deluxe Family PLUS"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe Family"),
            # suite (7)
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe Kriváň"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe Premium"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Premium Kriváň"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Prestige"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite President"),
            RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Penthouse"),
        ]

        mappings = self.matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
            hotel=hotel,
            booking_offers=booking_offers,
            direct_offers=direct_offers,
            global_rules={
                "min_fuzzy_score": 70,
                "capacity_priority_order": ["family", "suite", "2_plus_1", "1_pax", "2_pax"],
                "exclusive_direct_groups": ["family"],
                "normalization_stop_words": [
                    "izba",
                    "room",
                    "s výhľadom",
                    "bez balkóna",
                    "s balkónom",
                ],
            },
            existing_mappings=[],
        )

        self.assertEqual(len(mappings), 19)
        by_pat = {m["direct_room_pattern"]: m for m in mappings}

        # 1_pax: both 1-lôžková rooms pair with Malá izba (classified as 1_pax via bed_info)
        for name in (
            "1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande",
            "1-lôžková Premium Kriváň/Premium „Kriváň“ Grande",
        ):
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["capacity_group"], "1_pax")
            self.assertEqual(
                by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"],
                "Malá izba s dvojlôžkom alebo s 2 oddelenými lôžkami",
            )
            self.assertFalse(by_pat[name]["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # 2_pax: both Mini rooms pair with Izba Premium Mini s balkónom
        for name in ("2-lôžková Premium Mini", "2-lôžková Premium Kriváň Mini"):
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["capacity_group"], "2_pax")
            self.assertEqual(
                by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"],
                "Izba Premium Mini s balkónom",
            )
            self.assertFalse(by_pat[name]["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # 2_plus_1: all PLUS and s prístelkou rooms pair strictly within 2_plus_1
        expected_2p1 = {
            "2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS": "Izba Deluxe s balkónom",
            "2-lôžková Premium s prístelkou": "Izba Premium s výhľadom na hory",
            "2-lôžková Premium PLUS": "Izba Premium s výhľadom na hory",
            "2-lôžková Premium Kriváň s prístelkou": "Izba Premium Kriváň s balkónom",
            "2-lôžková Premium Kriváň PLUS": "Izba Premium Kriváň s balkónom",
            "2-lôžková Deluxe Premium PLUS": "Izba Premium s výhľadom na hory",
        }
        booking_2p1_names = {
            "Izba Deluxe s balkónom",
            "Izba Premium s výhľadom na hory",
            "Izba Premium Kriváň s balkónom",
        }
        for name, expected_booking in expected_2p1.items():
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["capacity_group"], "2_plus_1")
            self.assertIn(by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"], booking_2p1_names)
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"], expected_booking)
            self.assertFalse(by_pat[name]["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # family: exclusive direct, booking_room_name=None
        for name in ("2-lôžková Deluxe Family PLUS", "Suite Deluxe Family"):
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["capacity_group"], "family")
            self.assertIsNone(by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"])
            self.assertTrue(by_pat[name]["is_exclusive_direct"])

        # suite: all 7 Suite rooms pair strictly within suite
        expected_suites = {
            "Suite Deluxe": "Suita Premium Deluxe",
            "Suite Deluxe Kriváň": "Suita Premium Deluxe",
            "Suite Deluxe Premium": "Suita Premium Deluxe",
            "Suite Premium Kriváň": "Suita Premium Deluxe",
            "Suite Prestige": "Suita Prestige",
            "Suite President": "Prezidentská suita",
            "Suite Penthouse": "Suita Penthouse",
        }
        booking_suite_names = {
            "Apartmán s výhľadom na hory",
            "Suita Prestige",
            "Prezidentská suita",
            "Suita Penthouse",
            "Suita Premium Deluxe",
        }
        for name, expected_booking in expected_suites.items():
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["capacity_group"], "suite")
            self.assertIn(by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"], booking_suite_names)
            self.assertEqual(by_pat[name]["booking_room_name"], expected_booking)
            self.assertFalse(by_pat[name]["is_exclusive_direct"])


class TestCatalogParsersIntegration(unittest.TestCase):
    """Playwright-based integration tests for BookingParser and DirectParser catalog modes."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
        cls.browser_manager = BrowserManager(headless=True)
        cls.browser_manager.launch_browser()

    @classmethod
    def tearDownClass(cls) -> None:
        cls.browser_manager.close()

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.booking_parser = BookingParser(screenshots_dir=Path(self.test_dir))
        self.direct_parser = DirectParser(screenshots_dir=Path(self.test_dir))
        self.page = self.browser_manager.new_page()

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        try:
            self.page.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
        shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_build_catalog_booking_url_strips_date_params(self) -> None:
        """Catalog URL must strip checkin/checkout/group_adults/no_rooms params."""
        url = (
            "https://www.booking.com/hotel/sk/grand-permon.sk.html"
            "?checkin=2026-12-01&checkout=2026-12-03&group_adults=2&no_rooms=1&lang=sk"
        )
        cleaned = self.booking_parser.build_catalog_booking_url(url)
        self.assertNotIn("checkin", cleaned)
        self.assertNotIn("checkout", cleaned)
        self.assertNotIn("group_adults", cleaned)
        self.assertIn("lang=sk", cleaned)

    def test_booking_parse_room_catalog_and_bed_inheritance(self) -> None:
        """Verify catalog extraction with .b08850ce41, bed_info joining, and price=0.0."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <table class="hprt-table">
                <tbody>
                    <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                        <td>
                            <span class="b08850ce41">Dvojlôžková izba Deluxe</span>
                            <div class="ddaf087582">1 veľká manželská posteľ</div>
                            <div class="ddaf087582">1 rozkladacia pohovka</div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="hprt-table-cell-occupancy">
                            <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                            <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                        </td>
                        <td><span class="bui-price-display__value">210,00 €</span></td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                        <td></td>
                        <td></td>
                        <td><span class="bui-price-display__value">235,00 €</span></td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr class="js-rt-block-row">
                        <td>
                            <a class="hprt-roomtype-link">Apartmán Exclusive</a>
                            <div class="ddaf087582">1 manželská posteľ</div>
                        </td>
                        <td class="hprt-table-cell-occupancy">
                            <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                            <span class="bicon-occupancy"></span>
                        </td>
                        <td><span class="bui-price-display__value">350,00 €</span></td>
                    </tr>
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        catalog = self.booking_parser.parse_room_catalog(
            page=self.page, rules={}, hotel_name="Test Hotel"
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(catalog), 2)
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].room_name, "Dvojlôžková izba Deluxe")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].price, 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(
            catalog[0].bed_info, "1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka"
        )
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].room_name, "Apartmán Exclusive")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].bed_info, "1 manželská posteľ")

        # Also verify bed_info inheritance on sub-rows in parse_hotel_page
        dated_res = self.booking_parser.parse_hotel_page(
            page=self.page, rules={}, hotel_name="Test Hotel", requested_guests=2
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(dated_res.offers), 3)
        self.assertEqual(
            dated_res.offers[1].bed_info,
            "1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
        )

    def test_booking_parse_room_catalog_undated_ancestor_tr_bed_info(self) -> None:
        """Undated Booking layout without table.hprt-table extracts bed_info via ancestor::tr[1]."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <table class="undated-rooms-table">
                <tbody>
                    <tr>
                        <td><span class="b08850ce41">Izba Single Economy</span></td>
                        <td><span class="ddaf087582">1 jednolôžková posteľ</span></td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr>
                        <td><span class="b08850ce41">Izba Premium s balkónom</span></td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 manželská posteľ</span>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 rozkladacia pohovka</span>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    <tr>
                        <td><span class="b08850ce41">Suita Premium Deluxe</span></td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 veľká manželská posteľ</span>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 rozkladacia pohovka</span>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        catalog = self.booking_parser.parse_room_catalog(
            page=self.page, rules={}, hotel_name="Grand Hotel Permon"
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(catalog), 3)
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].room_name, "Izba Single Economy")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].bed_info, "1 jednolôžková posteľ")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].room_name, "Izba Premium s balkónom")
        self.assertEqual(
            catalog[1].bed_info, "1 manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka"
        )
        self.assertEqual(catalog[2].room_name, "Suita Premium Deluxe")
        self.assertEqual(
            catalog[2].bed_info, "1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka"
        )

        matcher = RoomMatcher()
        mappings = matcher.build_catalog_mappings(
            hotel={"id": "permon", "name": "Grand Hotel Permon", "matching_rules": {}},
            booking_offers=catalog,
            direct_offers=[
                RoomOffer(room_name="1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"),
                RoomOffer(room_name="2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"),
                RoomOffer(room_name="Suite Deluxe Premium"),
            ],
            global_rules={"min_fuzzy_score": 70},
            existing_mappings=[],
        )
        by_pat = {m["direct_room_pattern"]: m for m in mappings}

        self.assertEqual(by_pat["1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"]["capacity_group"], "1_pax")
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"]["booking_room_name"],
            "Izba Single Economy",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["1-lôžková Premium/Premium Grande"]["booking_bed_info"],
            "1 jednolôžková posteľ",
        )

        self.assertEqual(by_pat["2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"]["capacity_group"], "2_plus_1")
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"]["booking_room_name"],
            "Izba Premium s balkónom",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["2-lôžková Deluxe Kriváň PLUS"]["booking_bed_info"],
            "1 manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
        )

        self.assertEqual(by_pat["Suite Deluxe Premium"]["capacity_group"], "suite")
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["Suite Deluxe Premium"]["booking_room_name"],
            "Suita Premium Deluxe",
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            by_pat["Suite Deluxe Premium"]["booking_bed_info"],
            "1 veľká manželská posteľ + 1 rozkladacia pohovka",
        )

    def test_booking_parse_room_catalog_ancestor_div_and_xpath_call(self) -> None:
        """Verify row-based catalog extraction across tr, property-card, li and footer filtering."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <table>
                <tbody>
                    <tr>
                        <td><span class="b08850ce41">Malá izba s dvojlôžkom</span></td>
                        <td>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 jednolôžková posteľ</span>
                            <span class="ddaf087582">1 pohovka</span>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                </tbody>
            </table>
            <div data-testid="property-card">
                <div><span class="b08850ce41">Izba Mini</span></div>
                <div><span class="ddaf087582">1 manželská posteľ</span></div>
            </div>
            <ul>
                <li>
                    <span class="b08850ce41">Apartmán Kriváň</span>
                    <div data-testid="bed-info">1 extra veľká manželská posteľ</div>
                </li>
                <li>
                    <span class="b08850ce41">Izba Mini</span>
                    <span class="ddaf087582">1 manželská posteľ</span>
                </li>
            </ul>
            <footer>
                <ul>
                    <li><span class="b08850ce41">Mestá</span></li>
                    <li><span class="b08850ce41">Podpora</span></li>
                </ul>
                <div class="b08850ce41">Objavujte</div>
            </footer>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)
        catalog = self.booking_parser.parse_room_catalog(
            page=self.page, rules={}, hotel_name="Row Layout Hotel"
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(catalog), 3)
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].room_name, "Malá izba s dvojlôžkom")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].bed_info, "1 jednolôžková posteľ + 1 pohovka")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].room_name, "Izba Mini")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].bed_info, "1 manželská posteľ")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[2].room_name, "Apartmán Kriváň")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[2].bed_info, "1 extra veľká manželská posteľ")
        self.assertNotIn("Mestá", [o.room_name for o in catalog])
        self.assertNotIn("Podpora", [o.room_name for o in catalog])
        self.assertNotIn("Objavujte", [o.room_name for o in catalog])

    def test_direct_parse_room_catalog_without_token_api_post(self) -> None:
        """DirectParser.parse_room_catalog ignores token_api_post and reads static rooms."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <div class="catalog">
                <h3 class="room-title">Izba Standard</h3>
                <h3 class="room-title">Rodinný apartmán</h3>
                <h3 class="room-title">Izba Standard</h3>
            </div>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "method": "token_api_post",
            "catalog_room_selectors": [".room-title"],
        }
        with patch.object(self.direct_parser, "_handle_token_api_post") as mock_token:
            catalog = self.direct_parser.parse_room_catalog(
                page=self.page, rules=rules, hotel_name="Permon"
            )
            mock_token.assert_not_called()

        self.assertEqual(len(catalog), 2)
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].room_name, "Izba Standard")
        self.assertEqual(catalog[0].price, 0.0)
        self.assertEqual(catalog[1].room_name, "Rodinný apartmán")

    def test_direct_parse_room_catalog_extracts_hidden_tabs(self) -> None:
        """DirectParser.parse_room_catalog extracts rooms from display:none tabs, normalizes text, and deduplicates."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <div class="w-tabs-section">
                <h3 class="room-title">  Izba   Standard  </h3>
                <h3 class="room-title">   </h3>
            </div>
            <div class="w-tabs-section" style="display: none;">
                <h3 class="room-title">Rodinný apartmán</h3>
                <h3 class="room-title">izba standard</h3>
                <h3 class="room-title">Prezidentský\n\tApartmán</h3>
            </div>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "catalog_room_selectors": [".w-tabs-section h3.room-title", ".room-title"],
        }
        catalog = self.direct_parser.parse_room_catalog(
            page=self.page, rules=rules, hotel_name="Permon"
        )

        self.assertEqual(
            [offer.room_name for offer in catalog],
            ["Izba Standard", "Rodinný apartmán", "Prezidentský Apartmán"],
        )
        self.assertTrue(all(offer.price == 0.0 for offer in catalog))

    def test_direct_parse_hotel_extracts_direct_offers_from_room_cards(self) -> None:
        """Daily direct parse extracts room-level direct_offers when room_card_selectors set."""
        html = """
        <html><body>
            <div class="room-card">
                <h3 class="room-name">Izba Standard</h3>
                <span class="room-price">189,00 €</span>
            </div>
            <div class="room-card">
                <h3 class="room-name">Rodinná izba 2+2</h3>
                <span class="room-price">320,00 €</span>
            </div>
        </body></html>
        """
        self.page.set_content(html)

        rules = {
            "room_card_selectors": [".room-card"],
            "room_name_selectors": [".room-name"],
            "price_selectors": [".room-price"],
            "price_regex": r"([\d\s.,]+)",
        }
        result = self.direct_parser.parse_direct_hotel(
            page=self.page, rules=rules, hotel_name="Permon"
        )
        self.assertTrue(result.is_available)
        self.assertEqual(result.direct_price, 189.0)
        self.assertEqual(len(result.direct_offers), 2)
        self.assertEqual(result.direct_offers[0].room_name, "Izba Standard")
        self.assertEqual(result.direct_offers[0].price, 189.0)
        self.assertEqual(result.direct_offers[1].room_name, "Rodinná izba 2+2")
        self.assertEqual(result.direct_offers[1].price, 320.0)


class TestSyncPipelineAndCLI(unittest.TestCase):
    """Tests for ScrapingEngine.sync_room_catalog, runtime mapping application, and --sync-rooms."""

    def test_sync_room_catalog_and_cli_flag(self) -> None:
        """Verify --sync-rooms CLI flag runs catalog sync without needing dates."""
        args = parse_arguments(["--sync-rooms", "--hotels", "h1"])
        self.assertTrue(args.sync_rooms)
        self.assertEqual(args.hotels, "h1")

        with patch("main.ScrapingEngine") as mock_engine_cls, patch("main.ConfigManager"), patch(
            "main.BrowserManager"
        ):
            mock_engine = MagicMock()
            mock_engine.sync_room_catalog.return_value = 5
            mock_engine_cls.return_value = mock_engine

            code = main(["--sync-rooms", "--hotels", "h1"])

        self.assertEqual(code, 0)
        mock_engine.sync_room_catalog.assert_called_once_with(hotel_identifiers=["h1"])

    def test_engine_sync_room_catalog_orchestration(self) -> None:
        """ScrapingEngine.sync_room_catalog fetches catalogs, matches, and upserts mappings."""
        mock_config = MagicMock()
        mock_config.get_target_hotels.return_value = [
            {
                "id": "h1",
                "name": "Hotel One",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/one?checkin=2026-12-01",
                "direct_url": "https://hotelone.sk/booking",
                "rooms_catalog_url": "https://hotelone.sk/izby",
                "direct_booking_engine": "permon_direct",
                "matching_rules": {},
            }
        ]
        mock_config.get_scraping_rules.return_value = {}
        mock_config.get_room_mappings.return_value = []
        mock_config.upsert_room_mappings.return_value = True

        mock_browser = MagicMock()
        mock_page = MagicMock()
        mock_browser.new_page.return_value = mock_page

        engine = ScrapingEngine(
            config_manager=mock_config,
            browser_manager=mock_browser,
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,
        )

        with patch.object(
            engine.booking_parser,
            "parse_room_catalog",
            return_value=[RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Standard", price=0.0)],
        ), patch.object(
            engine.direct_parser,
            "parse_room_catalog",
            return_value=[
                RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba Štandard", price=0.0),
                RoomOffer(room_name="Rodinná izba", price=0.0),
            ],
        ):
            synced = engine.sync_room_catalog()

        self.assertEqual(synced, 2)
        mock_page.goto.assert_any_call(
            "https://booking.com/hotel/one", wait_until="domcontentloaded"
        )
        mock_page.goto.assert_any_call(
            "https://hotelone.sk/izby", wait_until="domcontentloaded"
        )
        mock_config.upsert_room_mappings.assert_called_once()
        upserted = mock_config.upsert_room_mappings.call_args[0][0]
        self.assertEqual(len(upserted), 2)
        self.assertEqual(upserted[0]["booking_room_name"], "Dvojlôžková izba Standard")
        self.assertEqual(upserted[1]["capacity_group"], "family")
        self.assertTrue(upserted[1]["is_exclusive_direct"])
        mock_browser.close.assert_called_once()

    def test_engine_sync_room_catalog_url_priorities(self) -> None:
        """sync_room_catalog prefers booking_rooms_url & rooms_catalog_url, falling back when None."""
        mock_config = MagicMock()
        mock_config.get_target_hotels.return_value = [
            {
                "id": "h_manual",
                "name": "Hotel Manual URLs",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/default-one?checkin=2026-12-01",
                "booking_rooms_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/manual-rooms?checkin=2026-12-01&checkout=2026-12-03&lang=sk",
                "direct_url": "https://hotelmanual.sk/book",
                "rooms_catalog_url": "https://hotelmanual.sk/izby",
                "direct_booking_engine": "generic",
                "matching_rules": {},
            },
            {
                "id": "h_fallback",
                "name": "Hotel Fallback URLs",
                "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/fallback-two?checkin=2026-12-01&checkout=2026-12-03",
                "booking_rooms_url": None,
                "direct_url": "https://hotelfallback.sk/ubytovanie",
                "rooms_catalog_url": None,
                "direct_booking_engine": "generic",
                "matching_rules": {},
            },
        ]
        mock_config.get_scraping_rules.return_value = {}
        mock_config.get_room_mappings.return_value = []
        mock_config.upsert_room_mappings.return_value = True

        mock_browser = MagicMock()
        mock_page = MagicMock()
        mock_browser.new_page.return_value = mock_page

        engine = ScrapingEngine(
            config_manager=mock_config,
            browser_manager=mock_browser,
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,
        )

        with patch.object(
            engine.booking_parser,
            "parse_room_catalog",
            return_value=[RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba", price=0.0)],
        ), patch.object(
            engine.direct_parser,
            "parse_room_catalog",
            return_value=[RoomOffer(room_name="Dvojlôžková izba", price=0.0)],
        ):
            synced = engine.sync_room_catalog()

        self.assertEqual(synced, 2)
        visited_urls = [call.args[0] for call in mock_page.goto.call_args_list]
        self.assertEqual(
            visited_urls,
            [
                "https://booking.com/hotel/manual-rooms?lang=sk",
                "https://hotelmanual.sk/izby",
                "https://booking.com/hotel/fallback-two",
                "https://hotelfallback.sk/ubytovanie",
            ],
        )

    def test_engine_daily_scrape_applies_saved_mappings(self) -> None:
        """Daily _scrape_single_hotel applies saved hs-room_mappings deterministically."""
        tomorrow = date.today() + timedelta(days=1)
        checkout = tomorrow + timedelta(days=2)
        job = ScrapingJob(check_in=tomorrow, check_out=checkout, adults=2)

        mock_config = MagicMock()
        mock_config.get_scraping_rules.return_value = {}
        mock_config.get_room_mappings.return_value = [
            {
                "hotel_id": "h1",
                "capacity_group": "2_pax",
                "direct_room_pattern": "Izba Classic",
                "booking_room_name": "Double Classic Room",
                "match_score": 92,
                "priority": 1,
                "is_active": True,
            }
        ]

        engine = ScrapingEngine(
            job=job,
            config_manager=mock_config,
            browser_manager=MagicMock(),
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,
        )
        mock_page = MagicMock()

        hotel = {
            "id": "h1",
            "name": "Hotel One",
            "booking_url": "https://booking.com/hotel/one",
            "direct_url": "https://hotelone.sk",
            "direct_booking_engine": "generic",
        }
        b_res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_name="Hotel One",
            booking_price=250.0,
            offers=[RoomOffer(room_name="Double Classic Room", price=250.0)],
        )
        d_res = ScrapedResult(
            hotel_name="Hotel One",
            direct_price=215.0,
            direct_offers=[RoomOffer(room_name="Izba Classic", price=215.0)],
        )

        with patch.object(engine.booking_parser, "parse_hotel_page", return_value=b_res), patch.object(
            engine.direct_parser, "parse_direct_hotel", return_value=d_res
        ):
            res = engine._scrape_single_hotel(hotel, mock_page, {})

        self.assertEqual(res.status, ResultStatus.SUCCESS)
        self.assertEqual(res.offers[0].capacity_group, "2_pax")
        self.assertEqual(res.offers[0].matched_room_name, "Izba Classic")
        self.assertEqual(res.offers[0].matched_direct_price, 215.0)
        self.assertEqual(res.direct_offers[0].matched_room_name, "Double Classic Room")

==================== FILE: ./tests/test_worker.py ====================

"""Unit tests for ScraperWorkerThread background execution and UI decoupling."""

from datetime import date, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import shutil
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

from core.models import ResultStatus, ScrapedResult, ScrapingJob
from ui_bridge.worker import ScraperWorkerThread


class TestScraperWorkerThread(unittest.TestCase):
    """Test suite for background worker thread lifecycle, queue dispatch, and error handling."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.test_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        self.output_dir = Path(self.test_dir) / "reports"
        self.progress_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()

        self.today = date.today()
        self.job = ScrapingJob(
            check_in=self.today + timedelta(days=2),
            check_out=self.today + timedelta(days=5),
            adults=2,
            children=0,
        )

        self.mock_config_manager = MagicMock()

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        if Path(self.test_dir).exists():
            shutil.rmtree(self.test_dir, ignore_errors=True)

    def test_worker_successful_lifecycle(self) -> None:
        """Verify non-blocking execution, queue message order, and report creation."""
        mock_results = [
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h1",
                hotel_name="Grand Hotel",
                booking_price=200.0,
                direct_price=180.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
            ),
            ScrapedResult(
                hotel_id="h2",
                hotel_name="Boutique Palace",
                booking_price=150.0,
                direct_price=150.0,
                status=ResultStatus.SUCCESS,
            ),
        ]

        worker = ScraperWorkerThread(
            job=self.job,
            config_manager=self.mock_config_manager,
            progress_queue=self.progress_queue,
            output_dir=str(self.output_dir),
            min_jitter_sec=0.0,
            max_jitter_sec=0.0,
        )

        with patch("scraper.engine.ScrapingEngine.run", return_value=mock_results):
            # 1. Non-blocking start
            worker.start()
            self.assertTrue(worker.is_alive())

            # 2. Wait for background thread to conclude
            worker.join(timeout=5.0)
            self.assertFalse(worker.is_alive())
            self.assertFalse(worker.is_active)

        # 3. Check report generation
        self.assertIsNotNone(worker.report_path)
        assert worker.report_path is not None
        self.assertTrue(Path(worker.report_path).exists())
        self.assertEqual(len(worker.results), 2)
        self.assertIsNone(worker.error)

        # 4. Check queue messages received
        messages = []
        while not self.progress_queue.empty():
            messages.append(self.progress_queue.get_nowait())

        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(messages), 3)

        # First message: starting
        self.assertEqual(messages[0]["status"], "starting")
        self.assertIn("Inicializácia", messages[0]["message"])

        # Finished message
        finished_msg = next((m for m in messages if m.get("status") == "finished"), None)
        self.assertIsNotNone(finished_msg)
        assert finished_msg is not None
        self.assertEqual(finished_msg["total_hotels"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(finished_msg["report_path"], worker.report_path)

        # Last message: idle (guaranteed by finally)
        self.assertEqual(messages[-1]["status"], "idle")
        self.assertFalse(messages[-1]["active"])

    def test_worker_failure_handling(self) -> None:
        """Verify that unexpected exceptions in worker thread are caught, logged, and posted to queue."""
        worker = ScraperWorkerThread(
            job=self.job,
            config_manager=self.mock_config_manager,
            progress_queue=self.progress_queue,
            output_dir=str(self.output_dir),
        )

        with patch(
            "scraper.engine.ScrapingEngine.run",
            side_effect=RuntimeError("Simulated unhandled driver failure"),
        ):
            worker.start()
            worker.join(timeout=5.0)

        # Verify worker recorded error and stopped
        self.assertFalse(worker.is_alive())
        self.assertIsNotNone(worker.error)
        self.assertIn("Simulated unhandled driver failure", worker.error or "")

        # Check queue received 'failed' and 'idle'
        messages = []
        while not self.progress_queue.empty():
            messages.append(self.progress_queue.get_nowait())

        failed_msg = next((m for m in messages if m.get("status") == "failed"), None)
        self.assertIsNotNone(failed_msg)
        assert failed_msg is not None
        self.assertIn("Simulated unhandled driver failure", failed_msg["error"])

        self.assertEqual(messages[-1]["status"], "idle")

    def test_worker_stop_signal(self) -> None:
        """Verify that calling stop() signals cancellation to engine."""
        worker = ScraperWorkerThread(
            job=self.job,
            config_manager=self.mock_config_manager,
            progress_queue=self.progress_queue,
        )

        self.assertFalse(worker.is_stop_requested())
        worker.stop()
        self.assertTrue(worker.is_stop_requested())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


==================== FILE: ./tests/__init__.py ====================

"""Test suite for hotel scraper."""


==================== FILE: ./main.py ====================

"""Autonomous CLI runner entry point for the Zero-Update desktop hotel price scraper."""

import argparse
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
import os
from pathlib import Path
import queue
import sys
import time
from typing import List, Optional

from config.settings import get_settings
from core.config_manager import ConfigManager
from core.logger import logger
from core.models import ScrapingJob
from scraper.browser import BrowserManager
from scraper.engine import ScrapingEngine
from ui_bridge.worker import ScraperWorkerThread


def parse_arguments(cli_args: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    """Parse CLI arguments with default options and validations."""
    tomorrow = date.today() + timedelta(days=1)
    default_checkout = tomorrow + timedelta(days=2)  # check-in + 2 dni

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Autonómny Zero-Update scraper cien hotelov (Booking.com vs. Priamy web).",
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--check-in",
        "--checkin",
        dest="check_in",
        type=str,
        default=tomorrow.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
        help="Dátum príchodu vo formáte YYYY-MM-DD (default: zajtrajší dátum).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--check-out",
        "--checkout",
        dest="check_out",
        type=str,
        default=default_checkout.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
        help="Dátum odchodu vo formáte YYYY-MM-DD (default: check-in + 2 dni).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--guests",
        "--adults",
        dest="guests",
        type=int,
        default=2,
        help="Počet dospelých hostí (default: 2).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--children",
        type=int,
        default=0,
        help="Počet detí (default: 0).",
        
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--child-ages",
        nargs="*",
        type=int,
        default=[],
        help="Veky jednotlivých detí oddelené medzerou (napr. --child-ages 6 alebo --child-ages 4 8).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--email",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Cieľový notifikačný email (voliteľné).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--hotels",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Zoznam ID alebo názvov hotelov oddelených čiarkou (default: všetky zo Supabase).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        type=str,
        default="reports",
        help="Cieľový adresár pre generované Excel reporty (default: reports).",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--headless",
        action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction,
        default=True,
        help="Spustiť Chromium prehliadač v headless režime.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--min-jitter",
        type=float,
        default=1.5,
        help="Minimálna stealth pauza v sekundách medzi requestami.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-jitter",
        type=float,
        default=3.5,
        help="Maximálna stealth pauza v sekundách medzi requestami.",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--sync-rooms",
        action="store_true",
        default=False,
        help="Spustiť bezdátumovú katalógovú synchronizáciu izieb a mapovaní do hs-room_mappings.",
    )

    parsed = parser.parse_args(cli_args)
    setattr(parsed, "adults", parsed.guests)
    return parsed


def main(cli_args: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> int:
    """Main CLI execution routine with full defensive exception handling."""
    logger.info("Spúšťam autonómny CLI runner hotelového scrapera...")

    try:
        args = parse_arguments(cli_args)

        # 1. Filter target hotels if specified
        hotel_identifiers: Optional[List[str]] = None
        if args.hotels:
            hotel_identifiers = [h.strip() for h in args.hotels.split(",") if h.strip()]

        # 2. Date-independent room catalog synchronization mode (--sync-rooms)
        if getattr(args, "sync_rooms", False):
            logger.info("Spúšťam katalógovú synchronizáciu izieb (--sync-rooms)...")
            config_manager = ConfigManager()
            browser_manager = BrowserManager(
                headless=args.headless,
                timeout_ms=get_settings().default_timeout_ms,
            )
            engine = ScrapingEngine(
                config_manager=config_manager,
                browser_manager=browser_manager,
                min_jitter_sec=args.min_jitter,
                max_jitter_sec=args.max_jitter,
            )
            synced_count = engine.sync_room_catalog(hotel_identifiers=hotel_identifiers)
            logger.info("=" * 60)
            logger.info("KATALÓGOVÁ SYNCHRONIZÁCIA IZIEB DOKONČENÁ:")
            logger.info(f"  - Počet synchronizovaných mapovaní izieb: {synced_count}")
            logger.info(f"  - Filtre hotelov: {hotel_identifiers or 'všetky'}")
            logger.info("=" * 60)
            return 0

        # 3. Parse and validate dates
        try:
            check_in_date = datetime.strptime(args.check_in, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
            check_out_date = datetime.strptime(args.check_out, "%Y-%m-%d").date()
        except ValueError as exc:
            logger.error(f"Neplatný formát dátumu (musí byť YYYY-MM-DD): {exc}")
            return 1

        # 4. Create validated ScrapingJob
        # 3. Create validated ScrapingJob
        try:
            # Extrakcia zoznamu vekov z CLI
            child_ages: List[int] = list(getattr(args, "child_ages", []) or [])

            # Fallback: ak používateľ zadal starší prepínač --children bez špecifikácie vekov
            if not child_ages and getattr(args, "children", 0) > 0:
                child_ages = [6] * args.children

            total_children = len(child_ages)

            job = ScrapingJob(
                check_in=check_in_date,
                check_out=check_out_date,
                adults=args.guests,
                children=total_children,
                children_ages=child_ages,
                email=args.email,
                hotel_identifiers=hotel_identifiers,
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
            logger.error(f"Validácia vstupných parametrov zlyhala: {exc}")
            return 1

        logger.info(
            f"Vytvorená úloha: {job.check_in} -> {job.check_out} ({job.nights} nocí), "
            f"{job.adults} dospelí, filtre hotelov: {job.hotel_identifiers or 'všetky'}"
        )

        # 4. Initialize ConfigManager, Queue, and BrowserManager
        config_manager = ConfigManager()
        progress_queue: queue.Queue = queue.Queue()
        browser_manager = BrowserManager(
            headless=args.headless,
            timeout_ms=get_settings().default_timeout_ms,
        )

        # 5. Launch background ScraperWorkerThread
        worker = ScraperWorkerThread(
            job=job,
            config_manager=config_manager,
            progress_queue=progress_queue,
            output_dir=args.output_dir,
            browser_manager=browser_manager,
        )
        worker.start()

        # 6. Monitor progress queue
        report_file_path: Optional[str] = None
        execution_failed = False
        failure_reason: Optional[str] = None

        while True:
            try:
                msg = progress_queue.get(timeout=0.5)
                status = msg.get("status")

                if status == "starting":
                    logger.info(msg.get("message", "Inicializácia zberu dát..."))
                elif status == "running":
                    curr = msg.get("current")
                    tot = msg.get("total")
                    hotel = msg.get("hotel")
                    pct = msg.get("percent")
                    logger.info(f"Priebeh: {pct}% | Spracovávam: {hotel} ({curr}/{tot})")
                elif status == "finished":
                    report_file_path = msg.get("report_path")
                elif status == "failed":
                    execution_failed = True
                    failure_reason = msg.get("error")
                    logger.critical(f"Chyba pri zbere dát: {failure_reason}")
                elif status == "stopped":
                    logger.warning(msg.get("message", "Zber dát bol prerušený."))
                elif status == "idle":
                    # Finished processing
                    break

            except queue.Empty:
                if not worker.is_alive():
                    break

        worker.join()

        if execution_failed:
            logger.critical(f"Beh scrapera zlyhal: {failure_reason}")
            return 1

        # 7. Verification of generated report and summary statistics
        results = worker.results
        successful_count = sum(
            1 for r in results if r.booking_price is not None or r.direct_price is not None
        )
        failed_count = len(results) - successful_count

        resolved_report = report_file_path or worker.report_path
        if resolved_report and Path(resolved_report).exists():
            logger.info("=" * 60)
            logger.info("FINÁLNE ZHRNUTIE ZBERU DÁT:")
            logger.info(f"  - Celkový počet hotelov: {len(results)}")
            logger.info(f"  - Úspešne vyťažené: {successful_count}")
            logger.info(f"  - Neúspešné / Nedostupné: {failed_count}")
            logger.info(f"  - Vygenerovaný Excel report: {resolved_report}")
            logger.info("=" * 60)
            return 0
        elif len(results) == 0:
            logger.warning("Neboli nájdené žiadne cieľové hotely na spracovanie.")
            return 0
        else:
            logger.error(f"Očakávaný report v '{args.output_dir}' nebol nájdený!")
            return 1

    except Exception as exc:
        # Critical top-level handler pushes fatal trace to Supabase app_logs sink
        logger.opt(exception=True).critical(f"Kritická neočakávaná chyba v main(): {exc}")
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

==================== FILE: ./supabase_schema.sql ====================

-- ==============================================================================
-- Supabase Schema for Zero-Update Hotel Price Scraper (hs_ namespace)
-- ==============================================================================

-- 1. App Logs Table (Centralized logging sink for WARNING, ERROR, CRITICAL)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.hs_app_logs (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    machine_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    level TEXT NOT NULL,
    module TEXT,
    message TEXT NOT NULL,
    traceback TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hs_app_logs_level ON public.hs_app_logs(level);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hs_app_logs_created_at ON public.hs_app_logs(created_at DESC);

-- Enable RLS & simple insert policy
ALTER TABLE public.hs_app_logs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon insert logs" ON public.hs_app_logs FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon read logs" ON public.hs_app_logs FOR SELECT USING (true);


-- 2. Target Hotels Table (Runtime list of hotels to scrape)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.hs_target_hotels (
    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    name TEXT NOT NULL,
    booking_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    direct_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    rooms_catalog_url TEXT,
    direct_booking_engine TEXT DEFAULT 'generic',
    matching_rules JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

ALTER TABLE public.hs_target_hotels ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS rooms_catalog_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE public.hs_target_hotels ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS matching_rules JSONB DEFAULT '{}'::jsonb;

ALTER TABLE public.hs_target_hotels ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon read hs_target_hotels" ON public.hs_target_hotels FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- 2b. Room Mappings Table (Pre-computed N:1 room catalog mappings)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public."hs-room_mappings" (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    hotel_id TEXT NOT NULL,
    capacity_group TEXT NOT NULL,
    direct_room_pattern TEXT NOT NULL,
    booking_room_name TEXT,
    match_score INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    is_exclusive_direct BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_manual_override BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    priority INTEGER DEFAULT 100,
    is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    last_synced_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    UNIQUE (hotel_id, capacity_group, direct_room_pattern)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_hs_room_mappings_hotel_active
    ON public."hs-room_mappings"(hotel_id, is_active, priority);

ALTER TABLE public."hs-room_mappings" ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon read hs-room_mappings" ON public."hs-room_mappings" FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon upsert hs-room_mappings" ON public."hs-room_mappings" FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon update hs-room_mappings" ON public."hs-room_mappings" FOR UPDATE USING (true);


-- 3. Scraping Configs Table (Zero-Update dynamic selectors, regexes, and rules)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.hs_scraping_configs (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    engine TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    rules JSONB NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

ALTER TABLE public.hs_scraping_configs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Allow anon read hs_scraping_configs" ON public.hs_scraping_configs FOR SELECT USING (true);

==================== FILE: ./instructions.md ====================

# Hotel Scraper Architect & Implementation Guide
A technical blueprint and coding guideline for developing a modular, zero-update desktop hotel price scraper.

## Core Architectural Rules
1. Zero-Update Philosophy:
   - Never hardcode CSS selectors, target hotel URLs, or timeout values in Python files.
   - Fetch all selectors, regex patterns, and hotel configurations from Supabase tables (scraping_configs, target_hotels) at runtime.
   - Support generic JSON-driven field parsing (fields_schema).

2. Strict Logging Protocol:
   - Absolute ban on print() calls. Use loguru.logger exclusively.
   - Implement a custom asynchronous/non-blocking Loguru sink pushing WARNING, ERROR, and CRITICAL records to the Supabase app_logs table.
   - Fall back to a local rotating file log (logs/app.log) if offline.

3. Defensive Processing:
   - Wrap every single hotel and field parsing routine in isolated try-except blocks.
   - An individual hotel extraction failure must log the incident and continue with remaining targets without terminating the scraping loop.

4. Thread Safety & UI Responsiveness:
   - Decouple the UI thread from the Playwright scraping engine using threading.Thread and queue.Queue.
   - Send granular progress updates (percentage, current hotel, warnings) to the queue for UI consumption.

5. Data Flow & Output:
   - Normalize inputs into an immutable ScrapingJob dataclass (frozen=True).
   - Export paired price comparisons (Booking.com vs. Direct hotel site, calculated delta) into an .xlsx file using Pandas/OpenPyXL.

## Module Structure
hotels_scraper/
├── config/settings.py          # Environment, Supabase keys, runtime paths
├── core/
│   ├── models.py              # ScrapingJob, ScrapedResult dataclasses
│   ├── config_manager.py      # Supabase remote rule retrieval & caching
│   └── logger.py              # Loguru setup with Supabase async sink
├── scraper/
│   ├── browser.py             # Playwright stealth context, viewport, headers
│   ├── engine.py              # Main loop, delays, queue dispatch
│   ├── booking_parser.py      # Dynamic Booking.com extractor
│   └── direct_parser.py       # Direct booking engine extractor (Profitroom, hoteladmin, custom)
├── exporters/excel_generator.py # Excel builder with delta highlighting
├── services/queue_manager.py  # Thread-safe queue communication
├── ui_bridge/                 # Worker management and frontend bridge
├── tests/                     # Verification tests
└── main.py                    # Application entry point

## Anti-Bot Best Practices
- Run Playwright with playwright-stealth.
- Inject randomized jitter pauses between requests (1.5s - 4.0s).
- Rotate standard desktop User-Agents.
- Intercept network responses (page.wait_for_response) where API or HTML fragments are available to bypass DOM overhead.

--------------------------------------------------
ÚLOHA:
Začni realizáciu projektu. Vytvor súbor `core/models.py` s nemennou triedou `ScrapingJob` (@dataclass(frozen=True)), kompletnou defenzívnou validáciou vstupov a štruktúrou pre výsledky `ScrapedResult`.

==================== FILE: ./README.md ====================

# Desktop Hotel Price Scraper (Zero-Update Architecture)

Autonómny modulárny scraper pre porovnávanie cien ubytovania medzi **Booking.com** a **priamymi webmi hotelov** (napr. Profitroom, Hoteladmin, custom enginy).

---

## Kľúčové architektonické princípy

1. **Zero-Update politika:**
   - Žiadne selektory, regexy, timeouty ani cieľové hotely nie sú natvrdo v kóde.
   - Všetko sa sťahuje dynamicky zo Supabase tabuliek (`scraping_configs`, `target_hotels`) za behu.
   - Pri výpadku siete existuje automatický fallback na lokálnu persistentnú JSON kešku (`.cache/config_cache.json`, `.cache/hotels_cache.json`).
2. **Striktné logovanie (zákaz `print()`):**
   - V celom projekte sa používa výhradne `loguru.logger`.
   - Záznamy úrovní `WARNING`, `ERROR` a `CRITICAL` sú asynchrónne a neblokujúco odosielané do Supabase tabuľky `app_logs` vrátane fingerprintu stroja (`machine_id`) a stack traceu.
   - Lokálny rotujúci fallback ukladá logy do `logs/app.log` (5 MB rotácia, 3 dni retencia, kompresia).
3. **Defenzívne spracovanie:**
   - Pád extrakcie jedného hotela (napr. zmena DOM, timeout) nezastaví celý proces – scraper incident zaloguje a pokračuje ďalším hotelom.
   - Automatické ukladanie diagnostických screenshotov pri neznámom layoute do `logs/screenshots/`.
4. **Thread Safety & UI Responsiveness:**
   - Playwright scraper beží vo vyhradenom worker vlákne (`ScraperWorkerThread`), čím neblokuje UI ani CLI.
   - Komunikácia prebieha cez thread-safe frontu `queue.Queue`.
5. **Anti-Bot stealth:**
   - Chromium beží s `playwright-stealth` a upravenými prepínačmi.
   - Náhodný jitter (pauza 1.5s – 3.5s) medzi požiadavkami.
   - Rotácia reálnych desktopových User-Agentov.
6. **Excel Reportér:**
   - Export do formátovaného `.xlsx` (Pandas + OpenPyXL) so zvýraznením delty cien:
     - **Zelené podfarbenie:** Priamy web je lacnejší (`Delta < 0`).
     - **Červené podfarbenie:** Booking je lacnejší (`Delta > 0`).
     - **Modré podfarbenie:** Rovnaká cena.

---

## Modulárna štruktúra

```
hotels_scraper/
├── config/
│   └── settings.py          # Konfigurácia, runtime cesty, .env načítanie
├── core/
│   ├── models.py            # ScrapingJob, ScrapedResult, ResultStatus
│   ├── config_manager.py    # Zero-Update sťahovanie pravidiel a hotelov s lokálnou keškou
│   └── logger.py            # Loguru konfigurácia a neblokujúci Supabase sink
├── scraper/
│   ├── browser.py           # Playwright Chromium lifecycle s playwright-stealth
│   ├── engine.py            # Hlavný orchestrátor, defenzívna slučka, jitter
│   ├── booking_parser.py    # Booking.com parser s detekciou vypredanosti (Sold Out)
│   └── direct_parser.py     # Parser priamych webov hotelov (Profitroom, hoteladmin, custom)
├── exporters/
│   └── excel_generator.py   # Excel reportér (Pandas + OpenPyXL) s farebným zvýraznením
├── services/
│   └── queue_manager.py     # Thread-safe zbernica udalostí a rušenie úloh
├── ui_bridge/
│   └── worker.py            # ScraperWorkerThread pre beh na pozadí
├── tests/                   # 57 unit a integračných testov
├── main.py                  # Autonómny CLI runner
├── supabase_schema.sql      # SQL schéma a seed dáta pre Supabase
└── requirements.txt         # Zoznam závislostí
```

---

## Inštalácia a spustenie

### 1. Inštalácia závislostí
```bash
python -m venv .venv
.\.venv\Scripts\pip install -r requirements.txt
.\.venv\Scripts\playwright install chromium
```

### 2. Konfigurácia prostredia
Skopírujte šablónu `.env.example` do `.env` a nastavte prístupy k Supabase:
```env
SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
SUPABASE_KEY=your-anon-or-service-role-key
```
*(Poznámka: Ak Supabase nenastavíte, scraper beží automaticky v offline režime z lokálnej kešky v `.cache/`)*.

### 3. Spustenie CLI runnera
```bash
# Predvolený beh (zajtrajší príchod na 2 noci pre 2 osoby v headless režime):
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe main.py

# Beh s vlastnými parametrami:
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe main.py --check-in 2026-12-01 --check-out 2026-12-05 --guests 2 --output-dir reports

# Výber konkrétnych hotelov:
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe main.py --hotels grand_hotel_kempinski,hotel_marrols

# Spustenie s viditeľným oknom prehliadača:
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe main.py --no-headless
```

### 4. Spustenie testovacej sady
```bash
.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m unittest discover tests
```
Všetkých 57 unit a integračných testov prebehne za ~5 sekúnd s kompletným overením všetkých komponentov.
